Spud the rig @ 00:00 (22.09.1393). RIH W/26"R.Bit (No.1) (W111), tag formation @ 15m. Start drilling formation to 95m. POOH to Surface due to low ROP, bit was balled up, RIH W/same

bit to BTM &amp;amp; Resume drilling to 157m. C.B.U, Run 20" 106#, k-55, BTC CSG to 155 m. Mix &amp;amp; pump 680 SXS CMT "G" SW:118 PCF, SV:170 bbls. Displace cement W/6 bbls mud (3bbls short

displace), after pump 150 bbls, cement got return (20 bbls return cement). Install 21-1/4", 2000 psi C.H.H. N/Up 20" 2k Single BOP &amp;amp; Hydrill. RIH W/ 17-1/2" R.Bit (No.2) (1320080JH) (1-

1-5) &amp;amp; Tag cement @ 155 m. Start D/out hard CMT, shoe &amp;amp; rat hole to 157 m.

Commence drilling formation W/17-1/2" R.Bit (No.2) F/157 to 312 m, while decrease GPM F/850 to 600 due to mud overflow from shaker. Resume drilling F/312 to 618 m W/NL-NG.

POOH to Surface as condition trip. Bit and Stabilizer was balled up. RIH W/same bit to BTM W/good hole condition &amp;amp; resume drilling F/618-625 m W/NL-NG. C.B.U due to pressure drop,

POOH to Surface. RIH W/17-1/2" MT.Bit (No.3) to BTM W/good hole condition &amp;amp; resume drilling F625-957 m W/NL-NG while increase MW F/72-75 PCF due to nearby well. C.B.U &amp;amp;

resume drilling F/957-1057 m. C.B.U, POOH F/1057-1000 m &amp;amp; run back to BTM W/good hole condition. Resume drilling to 1193m. POOH to Surface due low ROP while tight hole

@1001m &amp;amp; hard W/B.Ream @ this point several times. Bit was balled up, RIH W/17-1/2" R.Bit (No.4) to 1193m, while W&amp;amp;R F/480-500m &amp;amp; F/930-950m &amp;amp; 1150-1193m. Commence

drilling F/1193-1275m. C.B.U, Perform wiper trip prior taking log and running casing twice W/hard W&amp;amp;B.Ream F/1275-1100m &amp;amp; hard W&amp;amp;R F/1087-1275m at first time &amp;amp; W/hard

W&amp;amp;B.Ream F/1275-950m &amp;amp; Run back to BTM W/good hole condition at second time. Take CDR/GR log F/1263m-20" CSG shoe. Run 13 3/8" CSG (72#, L80, BTC) to 1274 m (13 3/8" CSG

POINT). Mix &amp;amp; pump 1960 SXS CMT (SV: 692 bbls, SW: 95 PCF) as lead and 434 SXS CMT CLASS”G” (SV: 105 bbls, SW: 118 PCF) as tail. Displace cement W/ 609 bbls mud (77 PCF). Return

60 bbls cement Check float, got return F/13-3/8" casing. Wait on cement 15 hrs to get hard. Install 21-1/4" 2K*13-5/8" 3K C.H.S, Energize P.seals &amp;amp; Test W/1500 psi, N/Up 13 5/8" - 5000

psi BOP Stack. Test BOP &amp;amp; chock manifold (all rams &amp;amp; chock manifold w/2500psi ann. W/1500psi). RIH W/12 1/4" MT.Bit (No.5) and tag cement @ 1208 m. D/out cement F/1208-1274m

and drilling formation to 1277m, displace well to 110 PCF mud and work on junk sub several times.

Start drlg formation f/2934m to 2936m, L/D bit &amp;amp; BHA, take 7” CBL-VDL log, wait on DD equipment, while wait on DD tools, RIH with 8 ½” 517 bit to 550m, Rec DD tools, POOH to

surface. P/up 4 ¾” D.H motor (TYPE:COUGAR, AKO=1.22 , SURF TEST OF DHM WAS OK) + PDC bit M1364 &amp;amp; RIH to 2936m. while Test MWD failed on btm, POOH to 1500, test MWD

was OK. RIH to 2937m (btm), cont drlg as slide &amp;amp; rotate mode to 3095m, perfrom condition trip, cont drlg f/3095 to 3170m, TDS IBOP failed, POOH to 2933m, fix TDS, RIH &amp;amp; cont drlg

to 3469m while pumping tandem pill ( lowvis-hivis), POOH due to low ROP &amp;amp; Check motor OK, L/D motor, test BOP, M/U new mud motor, RIH to 2933.5m ( 7” shoe), slip &amp;amp; cut, RIH to

btm, cond directional drilling to 3659.2m ( section TD). Pump 40bbl tandem pill &amp;amp; POOH as condition trip, good hole condition, RIH to TD, CBU, spot 50bbl HiVis pill, POOH to 406m,

wait on liner hanger, POOH to surface, L/D motor &amp;amp; DD tools, bit (1-1-WT-A-X-I-NO-TD). Wain on receive 4 ½” centralizer &amp;amp; stop collar, R/U liner running tools, run 4 ½” perforated

liner to 3659.2m. drop ball set hanger @ 2880m ( TOL), liner shoe @ 3658.5m. release setting tool, CBU cap of string, pooh to surface. M/U 3 ¾” E.mill, RIH to 2883m, D/O pack off

bushing, D/O LDC @ 2920m. cont RIH to 3068m, No GO. String held up, POOH to surface. L/D E.Mill, RIH with 3 ½” E.mill, RIH to 3068m, string held up, work on same, NO GO. POOH to

surface, M/U 3 ¾” E.Mill, RIH to 3068m, work on same, clear &amp;amp; pass point. RIH &amp;amp; Tag 4 ½” shoe, CBU &amp;amp; spot 153bbl Cacl2 brine 77pcf. POOH to surface. Run 7” bridge plug, set same @

2403m (inside 7” liner). POOH &amp;amp; L/D B.P running tool. RIH with Open end drill pipe to 2403m. Spot 10bbl, 118pcf, cement balanced plug on top of bridge plug. POOH to 2150, flush

string, WOC to get hard, tag cmt with 15kips, OK. Close BOP top ram, positive test to 1500psi in steps, OK. Displace 77pcf mud on top of plug with inhibited brine, POOHto surface. R/U

&amp;amp; Run dry test tools with 1500m water cushion, set packer @ 2280m, open hydrosping, monitor well, no bubbles, Close DCIP valve, drop bar &amp;amp; reverse with 45bbl mud, unset packer,

circ out, POOH to surface. RIH with Open end drill pipe, to 1600m, spot 35bbl 118pcf cmt as balanced plug, displace with mud, POOH to 1400m, WOC , tag hard cmt with 15klb,OK.

Close top BOP rams, positive test same by 1500psi, OK. POOH to surface &amp;amp; L/D tublar, install hand made plate on THS, Release RIG @ 24:00 O'CLOCK on 3/27/2015 (94.01.07).

7

Cont drilling 8 ½” hole size f/ 1719 to 1721m, displace hole with 74 pcf, mud, cont drlg to 1725m work on junk sub, POOH to surface, L/D bit, change top rams, perform 9 5/8” csg CBLVDL log, change top rams, M/U 8 ½” BESTE 1365 PDC bit, RIH to 1725m ( btm), cont drlg to 1739m ( top of Asmari), perform FIT with 200psi, cont’ drlg to 1948m, with 8-10bph loss,

pump 100bbl MICA LCM pill10 ppb, cont drlg to 2060m w/ 3-4bph loss, ( pump 5ppb MICA), CBU, condition trip to 1800m , RIH to btm, cont drlg to 2072m with 2-3 bph loss ( pump

5ppb Mica), cont drill to 2228m w/2-3 bph, cont drlg 8 ½” hole to 2380m 2-3 bph loss, condition trip to 2050m, RIH to btm, cont drlg to 2460m(KOP) with 3-4bph loss. POOH to

surface, L/D bit, run Sonic/CDR/GR log to 9 5/8” csg shoe, ([[MAX.DEV](http://MAX.DEV)]([http://MAX.DEV).=1.5](http://MAX.DEV).=1.5) DEG @ 1853m), test BOP, M/U DD BHA, P/U 6 ¾” (TYPE:COUGAR, AKO=1.5 &amp;amp; SURF TEST OF DHM &amp;amp; MWD

WAS OK). RIH to 2460m (KOP), test motor &amp;amp; MWD OK. CBU, D.Drilling as slide &amp;amp; rotate mode to 2628m (inc mw to 77pcf) with 2-3bph loss. Cont drlg to 2676m while increase MW f/

77 to 78 pcf. Due to obs. Oil in return mud. Cont drlg in slide &amp;amp; rotate mode to 2760m. Perform condition trip, cont drlg to 2934m (section TD, Liner point). Pump Hivis pill, Perform

condition trip, spot 40bbl hivis pill, POOH to surface. L/D bit (1-1-WT-A-X-I-NO-TD) &amp;amp; motor. Run 7” liner(29#,L-80,TPCQ) + 8 ¼” Hydraulic liner hanger to 2933.5m, drop ball set

hanger, shear ball seat with 1700 psi, release setting tool ( TOL @ 1658m, 7” liner shoe @ 2933.5m). R/U cement head, pump 20 bbl chemical wash&amp;amp; 150bbl 110 pcf cmt slurry,

displace with 247bbl mud, obs shearing @ 1500psi &amp;amp; DID NOT obs. Bumping. No cmt loss, POOH 10std wet, flush string, change top rams from 7” to 5”. P/U 8 ½” R.Bit (217), RIH to

1430m CBU, did not obs. Cmt on shaker, RIH to 1658m, Tag TOL, CBU, No cmt on shaker, POOH to surface, L/D 8 ½” bit. P/U &amp;amp; M/U 6 1/8” 517 B.Bit &amp;amp; RIH, D/O pack off bushing @

1660m. D/O plugs @ 2840m &amp;amp; hard cmt to 2880m. Pump hivis pill, POOH to 1650m perform Liner Lap positive test step by step to 1500psi, OK. Displace top liner with WTR inflow test

was OK. Displace hole with 78pcf mud, pooh to surface. L/D 6 1/8" B.BIT (0-0-WT-A-E-I-NO-DST). BOP test. R/U dry test tools, RIH with 852m as water cushion. Set packer at 1620m.

Open hydro-spring valve, No Bubble Observed. OK. Drop bar reverse circulation by mud &amp;amp; recover water on surface, unset packer, circulate thru string, POOH &amp;amp; L/D dry test tools. RIH

with B.Bit 517 to 2880m tag cmt, D/O hard cmt to 2933m (LDC @ 2895m &amp;amp; Float collar @ 2920m &amp;amp; float shoe @ 2933m). Clean out rat hole to 2934m.

string &amp;amp; exert torque, got free. POOH to 3708 m W/B.Ream &amp;amp; pumping, stuck string, pump 400 bbls 65 PCF

water while work on stuck down ward, got free. POOH to surface. Take RESISISTIVITY/SONIC/GR/CALIPER/CDR

log F/3600-2450m, POOH to surface, Take density/neutron tools F/3600-2450m, POOH to surface. Take CBL/VDL

log F/1842m to surface, Run CDR/GR tools to 3536m as forth run, break cable from cable head. POOH to surface,

left in hole CDR/GR tools (length of fish: 9.30). Assemble 5" overshot &amp;amp; RIH to 3752m, touch fish, work on same

to catch fish, POOH to surface and recovered fish completely. RIH W/ 8 1/2" used B.bit to BTM while W&amp;amp;R F/

3760-3906 m several times. Try to run gyro, no success. Wash &amp;amp; Ream TD to 3700m several times while string

stuck two times and got free. At 3894 m , [[R.I.ST](http://R.I.ST)]([http://R.I.ST](http://R.I.ST)) Gyro tools as Fourth run, from 3820 m, log up to 3000 m- POOH

to surface while Wash &amp;amp; B.Ream F/3894-3750 m W/ 3-4 BPH loss. stuck pipes @3750m, Pump 300 bbls 65 PCF

F.water work on stuck down ward and exert torque got free. W&amp;amp;R F/3750-3906 m (TD) several times W/3-5 BPH

loss, POOH F/3906-3750 m W/ 50-60 klbs drag up &amp;amp; 5-6 BPH loss, Cont. POOH to surface W/ NL-NG. Run 7" liner

(totally 84 JTS 7" liner 29#, l80, TPCQ) to 950 m. Continue RIH W/7" hyd. Set 1 hanger + drillable P.O.B + tie back

sleeve (all: 29#,l-80,P.P.A) tie back sleeve (ID: 7 3/8", length: 1.80 m) to 3805 m, stuck pipe @ 3805 m, work on

stuck upward &amp;amp; down ward , RIH to 3840 m , try to more progress no success. Mix &amp;amp; pump 173 bbls cement. As

lead (S.W: 100 PCF,) &amp;amp; 57 bbls (S.W: 118 PCF) as tail, displace W/ 355 bbls (82 PCF) mud, while observe Shearing

&amp;amp; Bumping. POOH to surface. RIH W/ 8 1/2" b.bit to 1718 m &amp;amp; tag 7" L.Lap, POOH to surface. Perform BOP test.

RIH W/ 6 1/8" b.bit &amp;amp; D/out P.O.B @ 1721 m. RIH to 3800MT(top of LDC),drill out hard cement to 3816MT,

POOH F/3816TO 1600m.perform injection test as totally pumped 80 bbl, got 25 bbl mud return. POOH to

surface, RIH with 9 5/8” RTTS to 1518m (set packer 200m above 7" liner lap), mix &amp;amp; pumped cement (S.V: 70 BBL,

S.W: 118 PCF), displace with 124 BBL 82 PCF mud ,Unset RTTS, POOH to surface, observed9 5/8" RTTS tools left

in hole which twist of from mandrill of circulation valve (length of fish : 3.95 m) estimated top of fish @ 1664 M.

Assemble 7 7/8" overshot &amp;amp; RIH to 1650m, recovered fish completely, RIH with 8 1/2" B.BIT to 1655m , drill out

cement F/1655 to 1700m. C.B.U to clean the hole. POOH to surface. RIH with dry test tools. Set packer @ 1680

m, perform dry test, It was Okay. POOH to surface. Running in hole with 6 1/8" B.BIT to 3816m, displace mud in

hole with 64 PCF water (PH:11), POOH to 1800m. Took CBL/VDL log F/3811M TO 1718M. L/D Logging tools.

Commence drilling formation F/1415 to 1423 W/ NL-NG. Due to low ROP stop drilling to change bit. RIH

W/12-1/4" PDC bit to 1423 &amp;amp; resume drilling F/1423 to 1587M W/ NL-NG. Found 3bbls increase in pit

volume, Weight up mud to 120 PCF, Cont. drilling to 1660m. Due to leakage from top drive’s saver sub,

POOH to 1600m &amp;amp; Replace it. Cont. drilling formation F/1660 to 1843m. POOH to surface ,L/D 12 1/4" PDC

BIT &amp;amp; stabilizer ,RIH W/ logging tools (CDR/GR/DT/CALIPER) to 1843m, log up to 1414m (13 3/8" CSG shoe),

POOH to surface, P/U &amp;amp; M/U 9 5/8" CSG shoe (47#, L-80,N.VAM), running 9 5/8" CSG (47#, L-80,TPCQ) to

1840m,wash down to 1843m,POOH to 1842m. R/U CMT line &amp;amp;Test to 3000psi. Pump 433bbls 130 PCF CMT

as lead &amp;amp; 61 bbls 118 PCF as tail, Release top CMT plug, Pump 6bbls 118PCF CMT &amp;amp; 6bbls solution, Displace

W/ 424bbls Mud &amp;amp; got 120 bbls CMT W/NL-NG. Install 9 5/8" CSG HGR, Test it W/1000psi. N/D 13 5/8"-5K

BOP STACK. Dress&amp;amp; bevel 9 5/8" CSG neck. N/U 13 5/8" 10K BOP stack. Test BOP stack. RIH W/ 8 1/2" NEW

B.BIT &amp;amp; BHA#7. D/O hard CMT, top &amp;amp; BTM plug F/ 1736 to 1840m. Cont. D/O F.shoe &amp;amp; Rat hole cement to

1843.

Commence drilling formation W/17-1/2" R.Bit F/157 - 240 m. Due to solid control system problems stop drilling to

fix that. Resume drilling formation F/240 to 521m, POOH F/ 521M TO 280M W/pumping&amp;amp; back ream .POOH to

surface, Bit &amp;amp;Stabilizer were balled up. M/U 17 1/2" new R.BIT. Resume drilling formation F/521 to 862m while

performed W&amp;amp;R on each STD. POOH to surf , fish (17 1/2" BIT + BIT SUB +2 JTS 9 3/4" DC'S, FISH LENGTH: 19.41M)

left in hole. RIH W/ 12 3/4"overshot, catch fish &amp;amp; POOH to surf, recovered fish completely. RIH W/ previous R.Bit,

W&amp;amp;R F/810 to 862 m. Resume drilling F/862 to 1082m,due to wash pipe failure stop drilling to fix that, Resume

drilling F/1082to1147m, POOH o surface while Hard W&amp;amp;R F/1110 to 600m,17 1/2" Stabilizer &amp;amp; Bit were balled up,

L/D 17 1/2" M.T BIT . RIH W/ 17 1/2" NEW M.T BIT. Resume drilling formation F/1147 to1415m W/NL-NG. Run

TOTCO &amp;amp; take survey. POOH to surf observed 17 1/2" BIT &amp;amp; Stab. balled up. RIH W/ 17 1/2" R.BIT, Add 1.5%

volumetric diesel to active mud system. POOH F/1415m to surface .Run CDR &amp;amp; GR, log up F/1415to1350m. Run 13

3/8" CSG 72 PPF, N-80, BTC to 1414m. Mix &amp;amp; pump 1663 SXS CMT "G" ( S.V : 851 BBL , S.W : 95 PCF ) as lead

cement &amp;amp; mix &amp;amp; pump 448 SXS CMT "G" ( S.V : 115 BBL , S.W : 118 PCF ) as tail CMT. Displace W/675 BBL MUD (77

PCF) without observation bumping while get 100 bbls CMT slurry return (W/ 95 PCF weight). Hang up 21 1/4" BOP

stack, drop 20"  *13 3/8" CSG hanger. Install SCG H.Spool 20 3/4" 3K*  13 5/8" 5K W/ 1NO. 2 1/16" 5K G.Valve on

each side &amp;amp; test same W/ 2500 psi. Test P-seals of C.H.S to 1500 PSI. N/U 13 5/8" 5K BOP stack. RIH W/ 12 1/4"

used M.T BIT &amp;amp; BHA#6 TO 1373m. D/O hard CMT &amp;amp;shoe @ 1414m &amp;amp; Rat hole to 1415m while work on junk sub.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

AZNS-118(G04)

Oil Field Development Project

E.Sequence of Operation

Pars Energy-Gostar Drilling EXploration Co.

Drilling Department

July 2016

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

26” Hole Section

E.1.1. General

Casing Point 20” Shoe @ 150mFormation AghajariRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems Bit Balled Up, Hole wash outDeviation control InclinometerLogging NilDrilling FluidPre-hydrate Bentonite MudMW 67-69 pcfVis. 40-70 secPV 10-12YP 35-40Gel 15/20PH 11Drilling ParametersGPM 300-900WOB 10-30 KLBsRPM 80-100

E.1.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 26” BHA as per BHA design. Tag formation at ±14m.

 Drill ahead formation down to 154M (20” casing point in Aghajari formation) using hivis bentonite pill. Pay enough attention to drill straight hole.

Note: TD should be adjusted to have Min 2m of rat hole below casing shoe.

- The exact TD will be determined based on casing tally.

- Run Inclinometer each 50m to evaluate the verticality of the hole.

- Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site and are in serviceable

condition.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section of hole are available.

- Enough Mud Material, LCM and Cement Should be Available before spud the rig to use

in case of needing.

- 1 Set pump truck should be available on well site before start drilling.

- Mud Logging unit should be available from rig spud.

- At TD, Circulate and clean the hole. Take Survey, POOH to surface. Check Bit.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 Perform a reamer trip due to conditioning the hole prior to run casing. If observe any

indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream to make sure that the hole

condition is good.

 Perform Safety meeting.

 R/U casing running equipment.

 Run ±150m (Approx. 15 Jts) 20", 94 ppf, K-55, BTC casing with duplex shoe to bottom

as per casing program. Casing shoe should be adjusted to have 1m of rat hole and enough

stick up (1 m on rig floor). Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud.

Note: Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or ledges are encountered.

 Run stinger on DP’s using the “C” Plate &amp;amp; 5” x 20” centralizer. Sting in. M/U &amp;amp; test

cementing lines. Circulate for one casing annulus volume. While circulating, check the

DPs annulus for any return. Use all DPs weight on the string if observing any minor

leakage through DPs annulus.

 Perform pilot test on cement &amp;amp; take sample from cement solution

 Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment regarding the corrected depth will

be done in due time. Displace with mud (1-2 bbl. Short Displace). Monitor for pressures.

Record and report the final pressure, Sting out. Check the float. POOH one stand,

Circulate to flush/clean drill pipe.

 After cement returns to cellar floor, open the 2" nipple on conductor &amp;amp; flush it.

 POOH to surface. L/D stinger.

 Keep the casing on the elevator until cement surface samples get hard. Check for casing

to be center inside conductor pipe.

 Note: Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to

perform top-up jobs.

- If there is no cement return to surface, a top-up job should be performed to fill the annulus.

Use same slurry weight as the primary cement slurry. Ensure the availability of adequate

X-over for connecting line pipe to cement line. The cement should be accelerated with

max. (2%) Calcium Chloride BWOC and no other additives. Wait for the top job cement

to get hard.

 Check Cement by slack off weight.

 Cut conductor pipe. Rough cut landing joint and L/D C.P. and L.J. Dress and bevel 20”

suitable to install and to weld casing head spool.

 Install and weld casing head housing 20-3/4” – 3000 psi on casing and pressure test the

sealing of welded casing. (Limit pressure to 400 psi a maximum of 80% of the collapse

strength of the casing). Outlets must be aligning with cellar stairs.

Note:

- Check the 20 3/4” wear bushing, 20 3/4” Plug Tester and 13-3/8” Casing Slip and seal

assembly in CHH before installing CHH (on Surface).

 If cellar is standard the distance between its top flange and ground level must be 1.73m.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 If cellar is not standard the base of CHH should not be more than 10cm upper than the

floor of cellar.

 N/U Double Studded Tie Down Flange 20-3/4” 3K x 21 1/4" 2K + 21-1/4” 2K Spacer

Spool + 21 1/4" 2K Double Rams and 21-1/4” Annular Preventer 2K and related lines

and valves and test all to 2500psi.

 N/U Bell Nipple &amp;amp; flow line

Note: The height of spacer spool must be designed untill the outlet of spool placed above the ground level.

 The rig should be centered again, before starting the next hole operation.

 P/U &amp;amp; M/U Next BHA and stand drill pipes to 1170m.

- Fill up the following forms and send to drilling office:

- 20” Casing Tally

- 20” Casing Running Report

- 20” CSG Cement Report

- 20” Hole section Summary

- Wellhead Diagram with Dimension Serial Number

E.1.3. Logging Program

No logging program is planned for this hole section.

E.1.4. Hole Deviation

Inclinometer

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

17-1/2 Hole Section

E.2.1. General

Casing Point 13-3/8” Shoe @ 1400 M, «Basal part of GS Fm.»Formation AghajariRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems Bit Balled up, Sticky formation, Low ROP, Overflow on shaker, Tight Hole, Washouts, Repeated Wash &amp;amp; Ream.Deviation control InclinometerLogging CDR/GR in AghajariDrilling Mud ParametersWater Base KCL polymer MudMW 70-80 pcfVis. 40-50 sec.PV ALAPYP 12-20 Lbs/100ft²PH 9.5-10Gel 3/6KCL 5%Drilling ParametersGPM 800-1000WOB 20-50 KLBsRPM 80-120

E.2.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 17-1/2” BHA as per BHA Design. RIH, tag top of cement. Drill out cement &amp;amp;

shoe and rat hole cement.

 Watch carefully for any indication of abnormal torque, since there might be a possibility

of shoe being backed off. Report problem immediately.

 Drill ahead formation down to 175m (20m below 20” casing shoe) with fresh 70 pcf Mud

as program.

 Circulate and clean hole, POOH to surface and P/U the same bit &amp;amp; BHA @ according to

BHA program, RIH to bottom.

 Drill ahead 17-1/2” hole section down to casing point @ ±1400m, at the top of Gachsaran

to case off full soft unconsolidated formation of lower Aghajari and drill the next hole

section with Higher mud density and flow zones.

Note:

- Adjust mud weight according to program and as hole condition dictate.

- Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

- The mud engineer shall check the mud properties as pre-planned time schedule

especially for (Ca++, THD and Chloride, pH, F.L, Gels, Mud weight and FV).

- TD should be adjusted so that to have 1-2 meters of rat hole for probable fillings, before

running casing.

- Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by well site geologist.

- Choke manifold and choke line must be lined up for SOFT SHUT-IN through pit or

burning line.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section are available.

- Ensure that enough amount and type of LCM are available and keep ready to mix and

pump LCM Pills to cure losses.

- At section TD, circulate, clean hole. Perform wiper trip to shoe and observe well @

shoe. RIH, Check for any fill. CBU on bottom. Spot 100 bbls hi-vis pill @ bottom.

 POOH to surface, if observed any indication of tight hole, run back to TD. W&amp;amp;R to

make sure that the hole condition is good prior to run casing.

 Perform pre-job safety meeting.

 Run Deviation Tool/GR Log in open hole.

 R/U casing running equipment.

 Function test float collar and float shoe at 13 3/8” Casing before running

 Run casing as per casing &amp;amp; centralizer program. Run ±1400m , 13 3/8", 68 ppf, N-

80, BTC casing.

 Casing shoe depth should be adjusted so that to have 1-2 meters of rat hole and enough

stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

Note:

- Ensure that the casing is drifted; cleaned and tallied priors to RIH.

- Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud.

- Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or ledges

are encountered.

 Perform pilot test on cement &amp;amp; take sample from cement solution

 When casing is at casing setting depth, circulate while preparing for cementing. M/U &amp;amp;

test cementing lines. Circulate for 120% of casing annulus volume.

 Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment regarding the corrected depth will

be done in due time. Displace with mud. Monitor for pressures. Bump plug with 1500

psi for 15min. Record and report the final pressure.

Note:

- Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made for top-up jobs.

 Hold on casing string weight. Drop casing hanger. Slack off 80% of casing string

weight. Check for proper installation. Pressure test casing hanger to 800psi.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 Remove the flow line and diverter. Rough cut L.J. Dress and bevel casing neck.

 Install CSG head spool 20 3/4”-3000*13 ⅝”-5000. Pack &amp;amp; Test p-seals and cavity Test

(75% CSG Collapse)

Note:

- Check the 13-5/8” wear bushing, 13-5/8” Plug Tester and 9-5/8” Casing Slip in CHS

and adopter flange before installing CHS (on Surface).

 N/U 13 5/8” x 10000 psi BOP stack. N/U bell nipple. Test BOP stack and related lines

to 7500 psi pressure, using plug tester.

 L/D 9 1/2” drill collars, P/U &amp;amp; M/U Next BHA and stand drill pipes.

- Fill up the following forms and send them to drilling office:

- 13-3/8” Casing Tally

- 13-3/8” Casing Running Report

- 13-3/8” Cement Report

- 17-1/2” Hole Section Summary

- Wellhead diagram with dimensions and serial numbers

- BOP’s Schematics with all related dimensions

- BOP’s test Report (each 14 days)

E.2.3. Logging Program

CDR/GR in Aghajari

E.2.4. Hole Deviation

Inclinometer (Single &amp;amp; Multi shot)

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

12-1/4” Hole Section

E.3.1. General

Casing Point 9-5/8” Shoe @ 1780 M «Cap Rock (Under G2)»Formation Gachsaran &amp;amp; Cap RockRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems Wash &amp;amp; Ream, S.WTR Flow and Inc. CA++ , Tight Hole &amp;amp; StuckDeviation control InclinometerLogging GR/CDRDrilling FluidParametersSalt saturated Kcl Polymer MudMW 115-125 pcfVis. 60 - 75 sec.PV ALAPYP 15-26 Lbs/100ft²PH 9-9.8FL &amp;lt;3Gel 4/8KCL 5%Drilling ParametersGPM 550-800WOB 20-40 KLBsRPM 110-180

E.3.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 12-1/4” BHA as per BHA design include Junk Sub. RIH, Tag top of cement. Drill

out plugs, Float collar, shoe track, (* After drill to 7m above float shoe, Perform Casing

Pressure test if bumping not observed) Displace hole with 115 pcf mud Clean out rat hole.

Clean out the rat hole, circulate clean. Work on junk sub.

 Drill 2m of new formation

 Perform F.I.T in Gachsaran Formation.

 Cont’ drilling 5m more of new formation

 POOH to surface, L/D junk sub and P/U new bit &amp;amp; string Stabilizer @ 20m, RIH to bottom.

 Continue drilling Fm. to Casing Point @ ±1780 M at the top of Gachsaran to top of Asmari

in cap rock.

Note: Perform BOP's test every 2 weeks.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

- Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

- Choke line and choke manifold must be lined up for Soft SHUT-IN.

- Adjust mud weight according to nearby wells and as hole condition dictation.

- The Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by the well site

geologist.

- Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site in serviceable condition.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools are available for this section.

- Ensure that enough amounts of LCM are available at location.

 At TD, circulate and clean the hole prior to wiper trip. Observe the hole for one hour @

casing shoe. RIH to bottom and Pump 50 bbl hi-vis pill, circulate to clean hole (check

bottoms up for any influx), Spot 70 bbl hi-vis Pill prior to POOH.

 POOH to surface. If observed any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream

to make sure that the hole condition is good.

 R/U wire line logging equipment. Run Wire line Logging as program CDR- GR.

 R/D wire line.

 Change top rams to 9-5/8”.

 R/U casing running equipment. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

 Check float shoe &amp;amp; float collar for holding &amp;amp; passing through.

 Run casing as per casing &amp;amp; Centralizer program

 Use Torque measuring System (TMS) for makeup casing, observe torque graph.

 Casing shoe depth should be adjusted for having short rat hole and enough stick up (1.5

m) on rig floor. Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud each 10 joints.

 Perform Pilot test on cement slurry, take sample from cement solution for testing

 M/U cementing head. Load the plug. WSS shall witness the loading. Test the lines to

3000 psi for 10 minutes. Make sure that the cement head is capable of holding pressure

while displacing and testing cement (Visually).

 Circ 1.2 times of casing volume with low GPM up to clean the shaker.

 Perform cementing as per program. Adjustment of cement volume should be done as per

change of drilling depth at operation time. Displace with mud. Bump plug with 3000psi

for 15min. (Max 80% Casing Burst). Record and report the final pressure. Bleed off

pressure. Check for back flow. If any back flow observed, hold the pressure inside casing

until the cement is hard.

 Hold on casing string weight. Drop casing hanger. Slack off 80% of casing string weight.

Close Rams. Check for proper installation. Pressure test casing slips.

 Raise BOP. Rough cut landing joint. L/D same. Dress and bevel casing neck

 T.H.S. 13 5/8”-5000 PSI x 11"–10000 PSI.

 Pack &amp;amp; Test p-seals and cavity test (Max 75% collapse)

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

Note: N/U bell nipple. Test BOP stack and related lines to 4500 psi pressure, using plug tester.

 L/D 8-1/2” drill collars, P/U &amp;amp; M/U Next BHA and stand drill pipes.

 Fill up the following forms and send them to drilling office :

- 9 5/8” Casing Tally.

- 9 5/8” Casing Running Report.

- 9 5/8” CSG Cement Report.

- 12 ¼” Hole Section Summary.

- Survey Report

- Wellhead diagram with dimensions and serial numbers, manufacturer.

- BOP’s Schematics with all related dimensions.

- BOP’s Test Report (each 7 Days).

- TMS DATA

- CDAS DATA

E.3.3. Logging Program

CDR/GR Log in 12-1/4” Open Hole

CBL/VDL/CCL/GR Log

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

8-1/2” Hole Section

E.4.1. General

Liner Point 7” Shoe @ 3934MFormationAsmari , Pabdeh , Gurpi , Ilam , Sarvak, Kazhdomi, Dariyan, GadvanRisks &amp;amp; Hole problemsTight hole, and stuck pipe/ Kick happened in Ilam Fm. in offset wells , Repeated Wash &amp;amp; Ream Heavy Mud Loss (Specially at Pabdeh Formation), Stuck pipe &amp;amp; loss in GadvanDeviation control MWD/GR from KOPLogging 9-5/8” SBT or RBT/GR + Full suite TLC logDrilling FluidCaCo3 / Salt Saturated KCL Base MudMW 75-81pcfVis. 35-45 sec (Use hi vis pill to clean hole)PV ALAPYP 15-22 Lbs/100ft²PH 9.5 - 10FL &amp;lt;3Gel 4/8KCL 5%Drilling ParametersGPM 300-500WOB 10-35 klbsRPM 80-120

E.4.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 8-1/2” B.Bit (135) w/ New BHA, RIH to top of 9-5/8” float collar (Top Plug).

 Drill out plugs, Float collar and cement to 2m above shoe, (* After drill to 7m above

float shoe, Perform Casing Pressure test if bumping not observed) Displace Mud in hole

with 75 pcf KCL Mud and Obs. The Well, Drill out remained cement and 9-5/8”. Float

shoe and cement of Rat hole, observe well for any Loss or flow. Watch carefully for any

indication of abnormal torque, since there might be the risk of shoe back off. In case,

report immediately.

 Drill 3m of new formation, work on junk sub, and observe well. C.B.U,

 Perform F.I.T to maximum section MW + 2 pcf

 Cont Drlg 10m of new formation.

 POOH to surface, L/D J.Sub.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 Run &amp;amp; Take CBL/VDL/CCL/Gr from 9 5/8” casing.

 P/UP New 8-1/2" PDC Bit and Drilling to 3585m (KOP).

 Consider bit life &amp;amp; POOH &amp;amp; change bit if bit ROP drop.

 Multi shot survey shall be run in this hole section before KOP.

 Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

 C.B.U and Clean the Hole, POH to Surface.

 Conditioning the hole before run log.

 Perform logging as program.

 L/D Wire line equipment.

Note: H2S services should be available 1 weeks before reaching ILAM entry point.

 Run D.DRLG Equip. and MWD/LWD/GR with New 8-1/2 PDC bit to 2350m

 D.Drilling F/ 3585M to 3934M (Max. Inc: 74° , Azi :40° , TVD: 3845.7 M -7” Liner

Point)

Note: Enough Mud Material &amp;amp; LCM Should Be Available In Well site.

Note:

- Adjust mud weight according to hole condition, use nearby wells as guide line.

- During trips, while at surface, driller is to check BHA.

- A material balance to determine the theoretical concentrations of additives in the mud

should be calculated and reported to WSS. Significant deviations from programmed

values should be explained.

- Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 7 Days.

 At section TD, CBU, sweep the hole cleaned, perform wiper trip.

 Spot 60 bbls of polymer high vis. pill to stabilize the hole prior to run log. Use 5-10 lbs

LCM (shell fish), if necessary. POOH.

 Condition The Hole before Running Full suite wireline/TLC Log.

 R/U &amp;amp; run fullsuite wireline/TLC log.

 L/D logging tools.

 RIH as condition trip prior to run 7” liner.

 Check wiper plug in LDC for Leakage. Check ball size in LDC.

 Perform Function test, flow through &amp;amp; hold test for float shoe &amp;amp; float collar in start of

running Liner.

 Run 7” Liner as per Program and Hang (1720-3934 M). 7”, 29 lb/ft, L80(1), Premium

Conn. , Float Shoe should be set 0.5 meters off bottom.

Note :

- Fill string w/ fresh mud (NO LCM) while RIH.

- 7”, 29 lb/ft, L80(1), Premium Conn. Float Collar should be installed one joint above

the shoe &amp;amp; LDC should be installed one joint above Float Collar.

- Bow Centralizer every 3 joints, 7” rigid centralizers on third and fourth joints from top.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

- 7” Hyd. Liner Hanger Must Be Suitable inside 9-5/8” CSG 47# with top packer.

7” Liner running procedure:

 After making up the shoe track, lower through rotary table and check that the floats are

holding. Fill up with mud and pick up to check float functionality. Circulate for 10 min.

 Run liner at controlled speed to minimize pressure surges (approx. 12 joints/hr). Check

returns constantly.

 Fill up liner completely every 10 joints run.

 When the required numbers of liner joints have been made up, make up the liner hanger

without Top Packer, running tool as per liner hanger engineer’s instructions.

 Circulate 120% of the liner capacity. (Record Liner weight)

 Run the liner with 5” DP to 9-5/8” CSG, Mud Circulation as 120% of String Cap to

check String, Liner hanger engineer should be on rig floor while running liner into open

hole.

 Use Torque measuring System (TMS) for makeup casing, observe torque graph.

NOTE : Check the loss while RIH if need Decrease the Speed of the RIH.

SLM &amp;amp; drift the drill pipe while RIH the liner.

 Continue Run to Bottom hole, Tag Bottom, 0.5 m Move Up string, Mud Circulation as

150% of String Cap.

 M/U combination plug dropping head to the last joint. Use pup joint, if necessary.

 The hanger will be set 64 m above the 9-5/8” Shoe.

 Try to avoid setting hanger on casing coupling. Use 9 5/8” casing Tally. Set the Hanger

as per hanger engineer procedure. Ensure that the running tool is free of the liner (work

string 0.5m up and down – record loss of weight as the hanger sets) before starting to

cement.

 Perform Pilot test on cement slurry, take sample from cement solution for testing

 Cement 7" liner as per cementing program.

 Displace the cement with the cementing pump to the liner wiper plug. Shear out liner

wiper plug (Record the volume pumped and shearing pressure). Displace combined

wiper plugs to latch down collar.

 Bump the plug with max. 3000 psi (Record and report final displacement pressure prior

to bump the plug). If there is no bump, do not displace more than 25% of the shoe-track

volume. Displace with Cementing Pump and record cement losses.

 Hold bumping pressure for 5 minutes. If the plugs bumped.

 If plugs bumped, pressure up and test liner to 3000 psi for 15 minutes.

 Bleed off pressure slowly and check for back-flow.

 P/U setting tool for two meters , check for U tube f/ DP &amp;amp; N/D Cementing Head

 While Cementing Mud Engineer must have level of Mud Tanks and announce any Loss

or return.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 POH to Surface While Lay Down Excess 6-1/2"DCs and 5" DPs on Floor.

 RIH with 8-1/2” R.Bit to 7” Liner Lap while D/O Hard Cement above it (if any) ,

Clean the Hole , POH to Surface.

Note:

If Cement Of above 7” L.Lap was Soft, WOC to Get Hard, then D/O Cement.

 RIH with 6-1/8” Bit and New BHA to 7” LDC while D/O P.O. Bushing &amp;amp; Circ , clean

out cement , D/O Plugs, LDC and CMT to maximum 2m above 7”. float collar (Touch

hard cement) . POH to 7” L.Lap , Pressure Test 7” L.Lap W/2000 Psi.

- -

If there is not any Cement Above 7" L.Lap Dry test should be performed and If Hard

cement was touched the decision will be made. If Needed to Perform 7” L.Lap Dry

Test, the Program will be Issued in due Time.

 If decided to Perform Dry test for top of Liner, with 2000psi differential pressure

(underbalance). Report the result in DDR.

- -

Do not forget to change top rams before running Log or each job apply corresponded

ram size and test bonnet to working pressure

- Do not forget to remove wear bushing before running log &amp;amp; liner.

- Do not forget to take Slow Pump Rate each 6 hours. Or when the mud properties

change.

- Over lap of 7” Liner and 9 5/8” Csg should be more than 60 m.

- Note: If procurement of the last hole section is not available on due time, do not drill

out 7” shoe &amp;amp; collar.

 Fill up the following forms and send them to the drilling office:

 7” Liner Tally

 7” Liner Report

 7” Cement Report.

 8-1/2” Hole Section Summary.

 BOP’s test report (each 7 Days)

 TMS DATA

 CDAS DATA

E.4.3. Logging Program

9-5/8” SBT or RBT /GR/full suite (TLC) Log

E.4.4. Hole Deviation

MWD/LWD+GR Data in Deviated Hole

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

6-1/8 Hole Section

E.5.1. General

Liner Point 4-1/2” Shoe @ 4284 MFormation Gadvan 5CRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems Tight hole, Losses / Flow , Repeated Wash &amp;amp; ReamDeviation control MWD+LWD+GRLogging/Test Full suite log TLCDrilling FluidSalt CaCo3 / Saturated KCL MudMW 81-82 pcfVis. 35-45 Sec (Use hi vis pill to clean hole)PV ALAPYP 15-22 Lbs/100ft²PH 9.5 - 10F.L &amp;lt;3Gel 4/8KCL 5%Drilling ParametersGPM 200-250WOB 10-22 klbsRPM 60-90

E.5.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 6-1/8” Roller Cone Bit w/ New BHA (Junk Sub Included), RIH to Top of CMT

&amp;amp; D/O CMT to 7”. Float collar (Top Plug).

 Drill out plugs, float collar and cement to 2m above shoe. (* After drill to 7m above LDC,

Perform Casing Pressure test if bumping not observed) Balance Mud in hole with 81 Pcf

KCL Mud and Obs. the Well Several Times, Drill out Remained Cement and 7”. Float

shoe and Cement of rat hole &amp;amp; 1m Below Shoe, observe well for 1 Hrs for any Loss or

flow. (Watch carefully for any indication of abnormal torque, since there might be the

risk of shoe back off. In case, report immediately.)

 If the well is static drill ahead to 2M below shoe, work on junk sub, observe well. C.B.U.

 Perform F.I.T to Max section MW+ 2pcf

 Cont Drilling 5m of new formation.

 POOH to surface, L/D J.Sub.

 P/UP 7" Scrapper, RIH to Bottom, Scrap &amp;amp; POOH, L/D Scrapper.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 R/U logging tools, Run SBT or RRT-GR Log inside 7" Liner to evaluate cement behind

casing.

 Perform TLC log for bottom section, if the logging tools don’t go to bottom &amp;amp; survey

around the 7” shoe. POOH &amp;amp; Log rest of interval with TLC method.

 POOH &amp;amp; L/D logging tools.

 P/UP New 6-1/8" PDC and Directional Drilling Assy. (Include MWD/LWD/GR) RIH to

BTM.

 Build &amp;amp; Keep Angle Drilling Ahead 6-1/8” Hole Section Down to 4284 M (Inc.: 90° &amp;amp; Azi:

40° , TVD : 3861M )

Note:

- During trips, while at surface, driller is to check BHA.

- A material balance to determine the theoretical concentrations of additives in the mud

should be calculated and reported to WSS. Significant deviations from programmed

values should be explained.

- Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 7 Days.

 At section TD, CBU, sweep the hole cleaned, perform wiper trip.

 Spot 50 bbls of polymer high vis. pill to stabilize the hole prior to run 4-1/2” Slotted Liner.

Use 5-10 lbs LCM (shell fish), if necessary.

 POOH &amp;amp; L/D BHA.

 Run Full suite log as TLC method. POOH &amp;amp; L/D logging tool.

 Perform condition trip prior to run liner.

 Prepare CSG Running , M/Up 4-1/2” Guide Shoe &amp;amp; Run enough 4-1/2” Slotted

Liner L80(1) 13.5 ppf BTC + 4-1/2” LDC L80(1) 13.5 ppf BTC + 2 Jts 4-1/2” Liner

L80(1) 13.5 ppf BTC + 4-1/2” Liner Hanger L80(1) 13.5 ppf (Single Cone hanger

C/W Top Weight set Packer) and Follow by 3-1/2” DPs &amp;amp; 5” DPs to 7” Shoe (3704

M)

 Circulation as 120% of String Cap to check String, Liner hanger engineer should be on

rig floor while running liner into open hole.

Cont. Run w/ 5” DPs to 4284M (Liner Hanger @ 3890 M), Drop Ball, The hanger will be set 44 M above the 7” Shoe.

 Pull out Setting Tools. POH to Surface While Lay Down Excess Pipe (3-1/2” DPs)

 RIH with 3-3/4” Mill and New BHA (3-1/2” HWDPs &amp;amp; 2-7/8” DPs) to 4-1/2” Shoe

(4284M) while Clean The Hole.

Note:

The Well Will Be Completed as a Gadvan Producer through 4-1/2” slotted Liner.

 RIH with 7" Scraper, Scrape 7" Liner to Completion Packer Setting Depth.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Sequence of Operation

 RIH with 3 3/8" mill &amp;amp; clean out inside 7” liner.

 Displace Mud in Hole w/Completion Fluid (Same as M.W) If the Well Is Static, POH

to Surface L/D All DPs and DCs.

 Run Completion string as program (Est. Completion PKR depth@ 3630 M)

 N/D BOP, N/U Well Head as Per Program.

 If well is not completed the suspension program will be issued at due time.

Note:

Do not forget to change top rams before running Log or Completion String. For each job apply corresponded ram size and test bonnet to working pressure

Do not forget to remove wear bushing before running log.

Do not forget to take Slow Pump each 6 hours.

 Complete and secure the well, Complete Wellhead and Release the Rig.

 Fill up the following forms and send them to the drilling office

 6-1/8” Hole Section Summary.

 BOP’s Schematics with all related dimensions

 BOP’s test report (each 7 Days)

 Completion String Tally

 Completion String Diagram

 Well Schematic

 Flow Sheet

 Well Handover

 End of Well Report (EOWR)

 TMS data

E.5.3. Logging Program

7” RBT-SBT/GR Log + Full suite log TLC

E.5.4. Deviation Hole

MWD/LWD/GR Data in Deviated Hole

 If well is not completed the temporary abandon program will be

issued at due time by contractor

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

AZNS-118(G04)

Oil Field Development Project

G. Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

Pars Energy-Gostar Drilling EXploration Co.

Drilling Department

July 2016

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

26” Hole Section

Hole Size(in)Casing Size(in)Casing Setting DepthMD (m)GeologicalFormatio nLithologyDrilling Fluids Design26 “ 20” 150 Aghajari Formation Marl, Sand-ShaleBentonite Mud/Hi-Vis Pill Gel

G.1.1. Drilling Fluids Formulation

Bentonite Mud/Hi-Vis Pill Gel has been planned to overcome the drilling problems and increase the rate of penetration. Rheological properties of the system will be increased and kept in the optimum range by adding BENTO-GEL API at the concentration of 20 – 30 lb/bbl.

Mud Type Bentonite Mud/Hi-Vis Pill GelMW (pcf) 65-68Funnel Viscosity (sec/qt)Active Mud &amp;lt;100Hi-Vis Pill = 110PV (cP) 7 – 10 ( ALAP )YP ( lb/100ft2) 30 – 35Fluid Loss (cc/30 min) NCpH 9.5 – 10.5MBT ( lb / bbl ) 20 – 30

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

26" Hole Size : BENTO-Gel Mud/ Hi Vis Pill (MW = 65-68 pcf)

Additives Material Description FunctionConcentrationAmount UnitDrill Water Drilling Water Base Fluid 98 % volCAUSTIC SODA Caustic Soda pH Control 0.5-0.7 lb/bblSODA ASH Soda Ash Hardness Control 0.7-1 lb/bblBENTO-GEL API Bentonite Viscosifier 28 lb/bblLIME Ca(OH)2 pH Control &amp;amp; Flocculant 0.5-1 lb/bbl

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of Drill water.

Note: Drill water should be tested before mixing and if its hardness is more than 400mg/l, it should

be treated.

2. Add 0.5-0.7 lb/bbl of CAUSTIC SODA to prepare brine to pH 10.5.

3. Add 0.7-1 lb/bbl of SODA ASH to control hardness in acceptable range below 400mg/lit

(To achieve better Ca++ settling, pH have to be kept above 10.5).

4. Add 20-30 lb/bbl of Bentonite and allow hydrating for as long as possible. Processing with a

shearing device will improve yield.

5. Add 0.5-1 lb/bbl LIME to system to adjust pH.

G.1.2. Interval Key Issues

 Loss Zone: There is a possibility of complete loss in this interval with No Return

mud, so LCM pill should be prepared before drilling of this section as contingency.

 Hole Cleaning: Hole cleaning problems could be avoided by proper use of viscosifiers,

e.g. BENTO-GEL API.

G.1.3. Engineering Outline &amp;amp; System Maintenance

 To achieve better Ca++ settling, pH have to be kept above 10

 The Plastic Viscosity value should be minimized by optimum usage of the solids control

equipment and by dilution with pre-mix, where applicable, to generate new volume and / or

reduce mud rheology.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

 Yield point should be kept high (the program range) to reduce the annular flow rates required

and gel value are required to reach to maximum hole cleaning.

 It is essential to remove drilled solids rather than dilute with water to acceptable levels.

Therefore every effort must be made to maximize the efficiency of the solids control equipment.

Where possible all centrifuges should be used to process the active mud and desilter discharge.

 Due to the hole size, and the likely pump rates available, a 6 rpm in excess of 12 is advised.

In order to achieve this, a quantity of pre-hydrated BENTO-GEL API will be needed in the

system make up.

G.1.4. Lesson Learned and Recommendation

These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they can be useful to drill this well if used.

 Appropriate GPM is required due to large hole diameter to provide good hole cleaning.

 Formation losses has been recorded in nearby well, so enough amount of LCMs should be

available while drilling.

Hole Size(in)Casing Size(in)Casing Setting DepthMD (m)GeologicalFormationLithology Drilling Fluids Design17 ½ ” 13 3/8 ” 1400 Aghajari, GachsaranMarl, SandShaleCLAY-BOND FLUID SYSTEM

Drilling Fluids Properties

To solve problems associated with this interval and increase the ROP, CLAY-BOND FLUID SYSTEM has been designed to drill the formation and inhibit clay and marl problems in this section.

CLAY-BOND L has also been successfully used to drill clay interbeds in both saline and non-saline systems. It is very important to determine MBT of mud during drilling and keep it in the program range. Controlling of MW could be done by running SCE and dressing the shakers with finest mesh screens, start with 68 pcf MW and increase every 100 meter 1 pcf using salts up to 75 pcf and then BARAWATE API up to 78 pcf.

Note: to serve a better hole cleaning, consider the following procedure prior to each trip and circulation:

 Pump polymeric Hi-Vis pill having funnel viscosity of 70 to 85 using 1.5-2 lb of BIO-XCD

and 1.5-2 lb PAC UHV.

ROP ENHANCER PILL

Mixing Procedure

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

1- Fill the tanks with 100 bbl of Drill water.

2- Add 65-70 lb/bbl of SODIUM CHLORIDE AC (NaCl).

3- Add 0.5-1 lb/bbl of CAUSTIC SODA to adjust pH around 10-11.

4- Add 2%vol ROP ENHANCER (2 Drum/100 bbl)

5- When ROP is low pump this pill or pump 50bbl of this pill every 100m.

G.2.2. Interval Key Issues

 Loss Zone: There is a possibility of complete loss in this interval with No Return mud, so

LCM pill should be prepared before drilling this section as contingency.

 Bit Balling: Due to the clay and marl characteristic of the formation, bit balling is one of the

main problems. It is required to use KCL R-UPG and CLAY-BOND L according to the above

mentioned to provide cutting encapsulation during drilling.

 Clay &amp;amp; Marl Inhibition: Tight hole might be experienced in this interval due to existence of

clay &amp;amp; marl in the formation. Good inhibition will be helpful to provide stable hole condition.

 Salt Water Flow: There is a possibility of salt water flow. Therefore close monitoring is

required to maintain Ca++ and pH level.

G.2.3. Engineering Outline &amp;amp; System Maintenance

 Mud weight to be kept between 68 and 78 pcf unless hole dictates, salts and BARA-WATE

API will be used to rise the mud weight. Controlling of MW could be done by running SCE

and dressing the shakers with finest mesh screens (150/ 200 mesh size) if possible.

 Dump and dilution is one of the best ways to keep the MW on the guideline especially with

clean mud systems. Running all solids equipment and dump and dilution would be the

best way to control the MW (after drilling each stand, 25 bbl can be dumped from

sandtrap).

 Rheological properties of the mud should be monitored to ensure maximum hole cleaning.

 Contamination with cement will increase the rheological properties of the mud especially the

yield point. Therefore, prior to cement, YP should be reduced to the minimum level if

practical.

G.2.4. Lesson Learned and Recommendation

These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they can be useful to drill this well if used.

 It is very important to determine MBT of mud during drilling and keep it in the program range.

 Running all solids equipment and dump and dilution would be the best way to control the

MW.

 Due to undesirable hole condition and hard casing running, regular wash and ream should be

performed on each drilled stand.

 Increasing mud weight smoothly, improve hole condition in previous well.

 Regarding high ROP, pre-treatment is a critical item while drilling this section.

Hole Size(in)Casing Size(in)Casing Setting DepthMD (m)GeologicalFormationLithology Drilling Fluids Design12 ¼ “ 9 5/8 ” 1780 Gachsaran FormationAnhydrite and clay stone, Marl, Limestone, with high pressure salt aquifer.Salt Saturated Polymer Mud

G.3.1. Drilling Fluids Formulation

To drill this section a Salt Saturated Polymer Mud with 115-125 pcf would be used. Salt would be used up to the saturation level (75 pcf) to prohibit the thin inter bedded layers of Salt in member 7 &amp;amp; 2 from being washed and also will act as a strong shale inhibitor in the system and maintain MW as per pore pressure curve dictates in the drilling program using BARA-WATE API.

Mud Weight should be increased 1 pcf every 30m up to 125 pcf, more than 125 pcf should be done with company man permission.

Rheology of the system would be raised by using BIO-XCD (Optional).

AMYLOSE LV, a modified starch would be used as a filtration control agent in this section. The main concern in this section is the effect of the formation water on the mud properties. If any problem rises during the drilling, AMYLOSE LV should be replaced with the PAC ULV at the concentration between 1-1.5 lb/bbl.

G.3.2. Interval Key Issues

 Salt Water Flow: Gachsaran formation has the potential of salt water flow. The hardness of

this water is between 35000-53000 mg/l, so any increase in the hardness of mud would be an

indication of possible salt water flow. Therefore, during drilling this section, hardness of the

system must be checked and monitored three times per day for any increase in Ca++ ions.

 Losses: In the upper part of Gachsaran a weak zone has been experienced. Layers interbedded

in the salts in Gachsaran Members 2 &amp;amp; 3 can have normal pressure, and therefore a reduced

fracture gradient compared to the salt layers. Heavy losses might be experienced in Gachsaran.

Losses have also been seen in the first shale layer of Cap Rock, its second layer from the top, so it is very important not to drill deeper than approximately one meter into this Member.

 Stuck Pipe: Tight hole / stuck pipe in the 12 1/4" hole section is typically isolated to the

Shales/Clay stones and salt beds. The stuck in Shales and clay stones as far as the MW is

adjusted correctly and FL is tight and the salinity is up to saturation is not a matter of concern.

G.3.3. Engineering Outline &amp;amp; System Maintenance

 Mud Weight

In the initial mixing, salt would be the first weighting agent up to 75 pcf, then BARA WATE API will be used to reach to 125 pcf as hole conditions dictate. Controlling the mud weight meanwhile drilling would be done by running all SCE and dump and dilution. Pore pressure is expected to be marginally above the normal pressure in the Jurassic / Upper Triassic.

Mud weight selection is critical in this interval. In some offset wells, excessive losses may occur in the Gachsaran member 7 when the mud weight is higher than the optimum. On the other hand, in some offset wells, water flow could be encountered when the mud weight is allowed to fall down below optimum MW.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

It is important to drill this section with the mud weight being kept as per the guide line, also by running all solids control equipments and dressing the shakers with finest mesh screens; it is possible to minimize the ECD and the potential for induced losses.

 Shale Inhibition

Salt would be used for shale inhibition to combat the Gachsaran Marl/Shale’s. However, the very tight FL would be the other shale inhibition property of the system. Controlling the salinity up to saturation is vital to drill into Gachsaran formation against troublesome Shales/Marls.

 Rheological Properties

PV is always to be kept as low as possible by reducing the drilled solids concentrations of mud using the proper installation of the solids control equipment so that the hydraulics is optimized.

High gel values are required to support the high quantity of weighting agents while tripping, making connections and during survey times. PV should be kept in the range mentioned in the drilling fluid program.

 A thin salt bed in Gachsaran member 2 is a red flag for Geologist that means reaching to cap rock

is imminent, salinity below the saturation will cause the salt samples to be dissolved in the system

resulting in no salt sample over the shakers.

G.3.4. Lesson Learned and Recommendation

These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they can be useful to drill this well if used.

 Due to undesirable hole condition while tripping and casing running, performing wash and

ream on each drilled stand is the best way to have good hole condition.

 Availability and running decenter centrifuge to remove LGS in this section is critical.

 It is important to drill this section with the mud weight being kept as per the guide line. Also

by running all solids control equipment and dressing the shakers with finest mesh screens; it

is possible to minimize the ECD and the potential for induced losses.

 A low as PV is required in this section to eliminate Mud Gel Up by suitable SCE layout.

G.4.1. Drilling Fluids Formulation

To drill the section, a highly Shale inhibited, ULTRA-INHIBIT FLUID SYSTEM has been design to pass through the highly reactive shale/Marl formation.

GLYCO-DRILL GP is a water soluble glycol helps to inhibit the reactivity of the shale in this formation. Temperature and salinity control the cloud point in this mud. The mud weight should be adjusted to 73 pcf using salt and raised up to 81 using CARBO-WATE F.

Note 1: Keep YP&amp;gt; 20 in Asmari Formation using BIO XCD and PAC UHV.

Note 2: Due to the importance of drilling this section, after drilling each stand in in shale and clay intervals the same stand should be wash and ream completely before connection and continuing drilling.

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of Drill water.

Note: Drill water should be tested before mixing and if its hardness is more than 500mg/l, it should

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

be treated.

2. Add 64.87 lb/bbl (20% wt) of SODIUM CHLORIDE AC (NaCl).

3. Add 3 % wt of Potassium Chloride (KCl R-UPG) and keep it in Asmari fm. then 20 m before Pabdeh

fm. increase to 5 % wt and Keep 5% till TD.

4. Add 1 lb/bbl of CAUSTIC SODA to prepare brine and 1 lb/bbl to treat system during drilling.

5. Add 1 lb/bbl of SODA ASH to control hardness in acceptable range below 500mg/lit and 1 lb/bbl to

treat system during drilling. (To achieve better Ca++ settling, pH have to be kept above 10.5 using

CAUSTIC SODA)

6. Add 8- 10 lb/bbl of AMYLOSE LV.

7. Add 1-2 lb/bbl of PAC LV-TG to control FL&amp;lt; 3 cc/30min.

8. Add 0.25 – 0.5 lb/bbl BIO-XCD/ PAC UHV to increase YP and allow hydrating for as long as possible.

Processing with a shearing device will improve yield.

9. Add CARBO-WATE F to adjust MW as plan and company instruction.

10. Add 1 lb/bbl ASPHA-SHALE NHP to system, keep it till TD.

11. Add 2% vol. of GLYCO-DRILL GP to system 20 m before Pabdeh fm., keep it till TD, and add 1%

vol. while drilling shale and clay intervals.

12. Add 1-2 lb/bbl of CLAY-BOND L to system, in shale and clay intervals add 1 can every 1m.

13. Add 1-2% vol of ECO-LUB HP to system during drilling as Optional ( in case of increase torque)

14. Add 4-5 lb/bbl of CARBO-WATE SF.

15. Add 5-10 lb/bbl of CARBO-WATE M to system 20 m before Pabdeh to form a thin and slick filter

cake in borehole and prevent the risk of stuck pipe (Optional)

16. Add 1.5-2 lb/bbl TORGUE LOW GR to system during drilling as Optional ( in case of increase torque)

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

G.4.2. Interval Key Issues

 Swab possibility

Special care must be taken when the BHA is inside the previous casing. In contrary to popular belief, pressure does travel easily in drilling fluids, so tripping speed must be controlled to avoid swabbing.

 Stuck Pipe

The combination of different size of CARBO-WATE incorporated into the mud will form a thin and slick filter cake in borehole and prevent the risk of stuck pipe.

 Drilling Issues

When drilling through fractured lime stones, swabbing of hydrocarbons can be experienced. Influx due to overpressure vs. influx due to swabbing must be differentiated.

 Losses

Pressure differences between the different reservoir zones in this section can cause lost circulation problems. It is recommended to drill with as low as possible mud weight through the reservoir.

 H2S &amp;amp; CO2 Gases

There is possibility for existence of H2S and CO2 Gases in the reservoir zones (Asmari,Pabdeh, Ilam and Sarvak), it is recommended to keep the pH around 9.5 to 10 and also using H2S SCAV P to prevent mud properties from being changed.

G.4.3. Engineering Outline &amp;amp; System Maintenance

 Mud Weight

A mud weight of 75-81 pcf will be used to drill this section, a mixture of Calcium Carbonate different grades will be used to reduce the filter cake thickness. It is preferred to use CARBO-WATE bridging agent rather than the conventional Calcium Carbonate.

 Rheology

Mud Rheology was justified to ensure maximum hole cleaning in both dynamic and static conditions. Using BIO-XCD/ PAC UHV would help to adjust the YP and 6/3 RPM.

PV is always to be kept as low as possible by reducing the drilled solids concentrations of mud using the proper installation of the solids control equipment so that the hydraulics is optimized.

YP is selected to be in the programmed range. High YP values are recommended to reduce the annular flow rates required and consequently less hole erosion.

 Shale Inhibition

To provide maximum inhibition during drilling this section, KCL R-UPG concentration should be kept at 3%-5% Wt and GLYCO-DRILL and CLAY-BOND L should be added before entering to the shale interval.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

 pH

During normal drilling operations, pH should be kept above a minimum of 9.0 in an attempt to minimize polymer degradation, to neutralize any H2S gas in the mud and to reduce the corrosion rate of the drill string.

 API Fluid Loss

This hole section will be drilled using ULTRA-INHIBIT FLUID SYSTEM. It is recommended to maintain the fluid loss below 3 cc/30 min using FL control polymers to minimize the filter cake thickness.

 Reduced Dilution Rates

GLYCO-DRILL GP improves the inhibition and stability of clays and shales and field data has shown that much lower dilution rates are required to maintain optimum mud properties. In some cases, the fluid can even be recycled to give further cost and environmental savings.

GLYCO-DRILL GP improves filter cake quality and reduces fluid loss as the cloud point threshold is exceeded, the micro-emulsion formed by the now insoluble Glyco-Drill will help to reduce the HPHT fluid loss. This improvement in filter cake quality and the lower fluid loss will significantly reduce the chances of differential stickin

G.4.4. Lesson Learned and Recommendation

These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they can be useful to drill this well if used.

 Previous Wells show rheological drop may be occurred while drilling Asmari Formation.

Keeping YP in desired range avoids probable washout in this formation.

 Gas and oil influx have been recorded in previous wells, mud weight should be designed to

prevent influx in Gurpi and Ilam formations.

 The combination of different size of CARBO-WATE incorporated into the mud will form a

thin and slick filter cake in borehole and prevent the risk of stuck pipe.

 High level of inhibition is critical while drilling Pabdeh, Gurpi, Laffan and Kazhdomi

formations. Sloughing shale has been recorded in previous wells.

 Due to the importance of drilling this section, after drilling each stand in shale and clay

intervals the same stand should be Wash and Ream completely before connection and

continuing drilling.

 It is recommended to maintain the fluid loss below 3 cc/30 min using the FL control polymers

to minimize the filter cake thickness.

Completion Fluid

G.6.1. Clean up and Completion Program

This program describes the procedure to clean up and displace the cased hole from water based mud brine as safely and effectively as possible prior to running the completion equipment.

Clean working practices and good housekeeping as always are important.

All rig pits, ditches, and lines (including gun lines) should be cleaned using solvents and detergents. If it is practical, lines should be opened to check for any mud solids that might have settled in them.

All the pits, sand traps, and under the shale shakers should be cleaned out and washed down using high pressure cleaning equipment. In the pit room, all gratings should be cleaned, all the lights and beams should be washed down.

All cleaning should be done well in advance wherever possible. To clean pits and lines, 90 bbl of water containing 1 to 2 drums of CLEAN SURF (as contingency) should be mixed up and circulated through all lines, gun lines, mixing lines etc. This can also be used to clean the tank system initially.

 Pump should be worked at the maximum safe rate.

 The below check list should be filled by the supervisor in the site.

Equipment CompleteBrine Storage TanksTransfer LinesPill and Slug PitsReserve/Mix PitRig PumpsTrip TankPump Charge LinesCement Unit LinesChoke Unit LinesKill Lines

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

 It should be noted that all dump valves should seal with grease to ensure they are in good order.

 All ditch gates should be sealed with silicon on each side of the gate. This will need to be

replaced if the gate is opened.

 UNDER NO CIRCUMSTANCES SHOULD BARITE, BENTONITE OR POLYMERS BE USED

TO SEAL ANYTHING.

 All pump packing should be examined before starting the cleaning.

G.6.2. Displacement Design

To ensure effective mud displacement and water wetting of the casing, the displacement pill needs to be designed carefully. The use of a weighted push pills with a viscosity greater than that of the mud, will minimize channeling and ensure good cleaning of the low side of the well bore.

The best fluid for thinning the mud is the continuous phase of the mud. In addition, SPERSEEN CFL could be used to reduce the yield point and gel strengths. The yield point should be between 8 to10 Pa and the 10 minute gel to &amp;lt;10 Pa. The actual quantity of material required to do this must be determined by the pilot testing performed by the mud engineer to determine the most cost-effective treatment.

Throughout the drilling fluid conditioning process, the flow rate used should be the maximum practical rate.

 The drill string should be reciprocated and rotated during the operation. The combined effect of

scrapers being run in the hole, the high flow rate and the reciprocation/rotation will help to

remove/disperse large quantities of mud solids and general debris. The reciprocation stroke

should be +/- 10 m. and the rotation speed should be +/- 60 RPM

 During this operation, as much of the surface volume of mud as possible should be removed from

the pits to accommodate the inhibited water and the well clean up pills. Once the mud has been

back-loaded, surface preparation can be done.

 At least 150 m of pill/spacer coverage or 3 min contact time at the highest pump rates should be

considered.

 Pit levels should be monitor throughout the displacement operation to catch and prevent an influx

entering the well contributing to an incident.

G.6.3. Completion Fluids Design and Formulation

Completion Fluid Formulation and Mixing Procedure

Completion Fluid : Inhibited Calcium Chloride Brine (80 pcf)Additives Material Description FunctionConcentration(lb/bbl) Gal/bblWater Water Base Fluid 273.4 33CALCIUM CHLORIDE DH CaCl2 Weighting Agent 173.08 -LIME pH Control pH Control 0.5-1 -BRINE-COR Corrosion Inhibitor Corrosion Inhibitor 3.5 0.4H2S SCAV P H2S Scavenger H2S Scavenger 1.8 -BIO-CIDE HP Biocide Biocide 0.4 0.04OX SCAV P Oxygen Scavenger Oxygen Scavenger 1.1 -

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of Drill water.

2. Add ±173.08 lb/bbl CaCl2 to the Drill water tank to get 80 pcf brine. (Mud Weight should

be monitored while adding CaCl2)

3. Add ± 0.5-1 lb/bbl of Lime for maintaining alkalinity.

4. Add 3.5 lb/bbl (0.4 gal/bbl) of BRINE-CORE (Corrosion inhibitor)

5. Add ± 1.8 lb/bbl of H2S SCAV P

6. Add 0.4 lb/bbl (0.04 gal/bbl) of BIO-CIDE.

7. Add ± 1.1 lb/bbl of OX SCAV P (to eliminate dissolved oxygen as a source of corrosion)

Note1: OX SCAV P addition should be done at the last stage just before Brine displacement in the hole. It should be mixed slowly to reduce the amount of air entrained in the brine and the amount of oxygen which may be incorporated into the system, also use of the mixing hopper and agitation of the completion fluid by surface equipment should be minimized.

Mud &amp;amp; Cement Program

Preparation before Cementing Job

1) Equipment inspection and maintenance.

2) Pump rate and other instrument calibration.

3) Water and dry cement preparation.

4) Cement additive preparation.

5) Pilot lab test with different ratio of drilling fluid and cement slurry using rig water.

6) High pressure lines and manifold connection with standby lines.

7) Submission of cementing program 24 hrs before cementing operation.

8) Pre-job meeting with all involved personnel and issuing the job procedure.

9) Double check all preparation jobs by every position.

10) Perform Pilot test on cement slurry, take sample from cement solution for testing.

11) Check wiper plug in LDC for Leakage. Check ball size in LDC.

12) Perform Function test, flow through &amp;amp; hold test for float shoe &amp;amp; float collar in start of running

Liner.

Drilling waste management plan shall be developed and implemented, and appropriate actions shall be taken to prevent environmental pollution, conserve resources and minimize waste. CONTRACTOR shall continually strive to minimize the impact of their WORK on the environment. Plans shall be enveloped where elimination of a pollution source is not practical, appropriate treatment of waste shall be undertaken and monitored to minimize the impact of discharges and disposals on the environment. The waste treatment and disposal shall be performed during the Drilling period. The system should consist of following separation, containment and handling equipment, and treatment and disposal.

1. Separation equipment: high efficiency solid and liquid separation system as described in

Schedule IV.

2. Containment and handling equipment:

 Conveyer: for collection and discharge of all cuttings.

 drying shale shaker: giving dryer cuttings and reduced mud consumption.

 Contingency tanks: Arrangement for the occasional collection, separation and isolation of cutting wastes.

 Fluid sump: for the collection of site drains and excess drilling fluid.

 Tanks: for the storage of excess fluid prior of treatment.

 Pumps: for transfer fluids.

 Backhoe, dump truck, etc. for the transportation of disposals

3. Treatment and disposal:

 Fixation: using cement or similar to stabilize the salt or chemicals.

 dewatering of excess fluids, site water, and water based drilling fluid.

Disposal area will be designated by COMPANY and CONTRACTOR shall transfer fixed cuttings to this location at CONTRACTOR‟s cost.

CONTRACTOR shall refer to the following standards for the effluent limits.

 IPS-E-PR-735 Engineering Standard for Process Design of Plant Solid Waste

Treatment and Disposal Systems.

 IPS-E-PR-730 Engineering Standard for Process Design Plant Waste-Water

 IPS-G-SF-130 General Standard for Disposal of Solid Waste.

 IPS-E-SF-880 General Standard for Water Pollution Control.

Waste Treatment Plan

Drilling waste management plan is the ideal and best practice for minimizing, recycling and disposal of drilling waste. Drilling Waste Management is planned based on the major produced wastes during the drilling operation. These wastes are

 Waste Water / Contaminated Water in the pit

 Drilling fluids

 Drilling Cuttings

The minimum requirement for implementing this plan is as below;

1- Improving Solids Control Efficiency and Monitoring

Dewatering Process Advantages

 60% to 85% Recovery of the aqueous phases from the waste mud

 40% reduction of waste volume and transportation.

 The water phase contained into the waste volume is almost totally recovered and resent back to the rig for new mud mixing, rig activities etc. This will drastically reduce

the cost of transportation for the water needed for operations.

 Reduction in waste pit size and smallest foot print

 Produced water should be tested for different ions.

 Dispose water from rig site shall be clear, Ca&amp;lt;800 mg/lit, 7&amp;lt;PH&amp;lt;8

Cutting Transfer System

Cuttings transfer system is required to collect all the solids discharge from solids control equipment. This system is installed under Shale Shaker and Centrifuge Area.

Cutting Treatment Process

Cuttings treatment is important part of the waste management services which is usually done to solidification and stabilize and fix the liquid phase movement on the solids.

Well : AZNS-118 (G04)

Type : IGH

WASTE MANAGEMENT PROGRAM Page 140 of 175

Development Well

Solidification refers to techniques that encapsulate the waste in a monolithic solid of high structural integrity. In this treatment, material is added to reduce free water and possibly reduce or slow potential contaminant release. Contaminant migration is restricted by vastly decreasing the surface area exposed to leaching by isolating the wastes within an impervious capsule.

Stabilization refers to those techniques that reduce the hazard potential of wastes by converting contaminants into their least soluble, mobile, or toxic form. The physical nature and handling characteristics of the waste are not necessarily changed by stabilization.
