12 1/4" Hole Section: 12-1/4” hole section was started from 1410 with 84 pcf KCL/Polymer mud. FIT was

performed @ 1413m &amp;amp; EMW was 90.5 pcf. MW was decreased to 77 pcf. Due to Observe a dramatic drop in

RPM from 160 to 70 &amp;amp; Torque increase significantly (15 KLB.FT) during drilling (1460m), Drilling stop &amp;amp;

work on it, No Succ. POOH to surface &amp;amp; use DHM (AKO=0). The mentioned problem observes two times.

Because of tight hole, MW increase to 81 pcf gradually. Twist off the IBOP and left in the hole with 3748m

fish, Catch with Over Shot. During drilling from 3745m to 3887m, string vibrate markedly in higher RPM

(higher than 80) &amp;amp; it's cause to drilling wase done with low RPM &amp;amp; it makes Low ROP. Drilling was

continued to 3905m (9 5/8" CSG shoe) &amp;amp; after condition trip, Full Suite log was perormed F/3905 to 1410m

(ZDL/CNL/DSL/DLL/MSFL/GR/DT). 9-5/8" casing string was run from surface to 3905m (358 Jts, T-95,

53.5 ppf,TPCQ). After pumping pre-flush (30 bbl water &amp;amp; 80 bbl O-chemicalwash) 20 bbl spacer (115 pcf),

CMT was mixed &amp;amp; pumped as well as displaced by 902 bbl mud (81 pcf). Bumping was observed (2300 psi). 40

bbl. contaminated &amp;amp; 200 bbl net CMT slurry was returned to surface. 13-5/8" 5K X 11" 10K T.H.S was

installed. Run 8 1/2" bit and tag TOC @ 3865m. Drill out cement plug, float collar &amp;amp; cement below the float

collar to 3882 m. Pressure test 9 5/8" casing string with 2500psi. D/Out to 3905m.

ZDL/CNL/DSL/DLL/MSFL/GR/DT &amp;amp; CBL/VDL/CCL/GR

8 1/2" Hole Section: 8 1/2” hole section was started from 3905 with 101 pcf KCL/Polymer mud. FIT was

performed @ 3909m &amp;amp; EMW was 114.6 pcf. Drilling was continued to 3936m &amp;amp; CBL/VDL/CCL/GR log was

performed F/ 3905 to surface. The mentioned problem in pervious section about increase Torque &amp;amp; decrease

RPM was observed. While drilling (4043m) 10% -70% gas on mud was detected, so MW increase from 101

to 105 after that content of gas in mud is between 0-10% . Drilling was continued to 4170m. Full suite log

was performed in three run (Run#1:BCS/DLL/MSFL/GR F/4170 TO 3905m , Run#2:SDL/DSN/CSNG

F/4170 TO 3905m &amp;amp; RUN#3: CDR/GR/DSI TOOK WEIGHT IN 3912m, try to pass. no succ. POOH to

surface). 7" liner (32 Jts, L-80,35 ppf, HYDRILL) was run from 3784.9m to 4170m (Liner shoe). After

pumping 15 bbl salt saturated water as spacer , CMT was mixed &amp;amp; pumped. Shearing was observed w/2000

psi, but Bumping wasn't observed. check back flow, OK. Run 8 1/2" bit and tag TOC @ 3618m. Drill out

cement to 3784.9m. The shortage of completion string &amp;amp; Acc. causes to suspended the well with three

cement plugs (Plug#1:3457m-3685m, Plug#2:2384m-2500m, Plug#3:1277m-1400m).

2. Lesson learned

Most of problems in south Azadegan field occurred in 3th and 4th interlayer of Gachsaran Group due to presence of sticky Red and Brown Marl. Also, reorganization of first interlayer of Gachsaran which into account cap rock of Asmari reservoir is so important to prevent lost and stuck problem through low pressure pore formation pressure.

Further shale and clay swelling in Bangestan Group is customary.

Understanding and anticipating drilling problems, understanding their causes, and planning solutions are necessary for overall-well-cost control and for successfully reaching the target zone. According to report of nearby wells, the following items has been considered to drilling the new well:

Bit balling is common problem. A rough bit surface will increase the surface area and increase the adhesive forces. When the mud flow stops, or if a bit with poor hydraulic design is used, an electrostatic force may cause clays to stick in the bit surface. With several work such as increase flow rate, Pump a caustic pill, Spin the bit as fast as possible can be prevented this problem.

Stuck is another problem in the previous wells. Stuck can be classified under two categories: differential and mechanical. In the nearby wells, it can be said that Mechanical type wasn’t happened except stuck 13 3/8” casing in AZN 047 at 765m (Aghajari formation and got free with 250 Klbs overpull). More stuck in the mentioned wells often is associated with complete loss, so differential type may be happened. In AZN 047, string got stuck with 71 PCF mud from 1928m to 1945m (Pabdeh formation) and got free with 40 Klbs Over pull. Another stuck in this well was happened with 76 PCF mud at 2675m to 2714m (Sarvak Formation) and got free with 60-100 Klbs over pull.

Tight hole is a common problem in the mentioned wells and usally for curing this problem, mud weight is increased. The serius tight hole was reported in Jahrum/Pabdeh and Gurpi. It should be noted that when tight hole is cured, mud weight don’t excess from critical value.

The costly problem that may be happened in future wells is twist off string. Twisting off is ocurred when the drill string parts by failing catastrophically under the torsional stress. This may happen if the drill string has been encountered either fatigue or excessive torque. This problem has been occurred four times in AZN 025(2660m, 3445m, 3472m and 3542m) and fish was recovered. Because of string got

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 45

stuck, twist off bit sub was happened in Jahrum/Pabdeh formation. Due to fish hasn’t been recovered; side tracking was performing from 1533m.

17 ½” Problems and Solutions:

In 17 ½″ hole section that had been drilled Aghajari-Gachsaran formation, tight hole was encounter when during the condition trip, wash and ream several times this interval. Totally operation is smoothly, a proper mud weight in this section is important for the well controlling operations. Also Hi.Vis pill pumping make the hoe condition better. This problem had been occurred in two situations:

 Bite toot wearing: if this problem depended on bit wearing should redisigned the bit

selection proucedure and review the bit performance history in this interval.adidithnally

in rather ran short drilling test and calculated Af for next bit selection

 Shale and saltted formation: If tight hole occurred under shale and clay sweilling or

saltted formation influence, should increase the mud wight to prevent pipe stuck.

 Thickness of mud cake: sometimes uncontrled grownig the mud cack is a factor cuase

to tight hole problem. The signe of this condithion is decreasing the sample on mud

returned. To solve this problem must contole water loos.

In other hand, increasing the Ca++ near the 1100m in Gachsaran formation occurred that solved in drilling opration by increasing mud weight. Since as important factor which influence on mud reology and espically on plastic vicosity is hole cleaning from drilling cutting. If cutting removing don’t as suffienet, cuttings remained in bore hole and redrilled by bit and be smaller. Smaller cutting cuase to increas the active surfaces and tottaly increas friction force between side surface and increasing plastic viscosity. The treat of this problem seprat into two way; solid control system included cenrefigue and chimacal treatment. In chemical treatment for removing Ca++ and Mg++ should be use of Posphat Complex and Ligno Sulfanite as Emulusifiers or Ditution the mud.

12 ¼” Problems and Solutions:

In 12 ¼ ″ hole section that had been drilled Gachsaran formation, lost circulation in 2800m-2900m interval happened and solved by LCM squeezing and Hi.Vis pumping. About lost circulation the main

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 46

mud with water and addetives in first step should recognize which kind of losting categories happent by mud levling from pumped and retuned mud tanks. Referr to this table:

To 25 bph Speeage Pourose and permeable media like sandstones formation25-100 bph partial Coars, sands and Gravel formationUp to 100 bph Sever Fractured and faulted formation

 If cuold not perevent the lost by LCM can use of magne-set program.

 For sever lost circulatio should use cementing but not in reservoir becuease

influenced on important factor of of reservoir specification which is permeability.

 It is recommended that the pump rate is no more than 500gpm in formations which

are vulnerable to lost circulation such as Asmari and Gurpi. Meanwhile, mud

weight shall not be changed sharply.

One of the dangerous problems that occurred during drilling this section is observing cut mud weight in 2200m to 2400 m interval. This problem has been solved by increasing the mud weight and circulated to balance it.

Bit balling is another problem that happened during drilling 17 ½″ and 12 ¼″ hole section in Aghajari and Gachsaran formation. The best approach is to use good fundamentals: an inhibitive mud, optimal hydraulics and a quality ROP enhancer. Cure bit balling by following items:

 Work the bit by rotating fast off bottom and reciprocating the string to shake off the

clay coating.

 Reduce ROP for a short time.

 Spot a walnut pill.

 Circulation.

 Fresh water.

 If bit balling is observed while drilling, a detergent is usually added to disperse the

balled clays.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 47

 The use of a small percentage of glycol also helps to reduce bit balling.

 Consider fresh water pill.

8 ½" Problems and Solutions:

Kick problem occurs in Gadvan formation near the 4000m to 4640m and solved by increasing the mud weight. For solving this problem, Recalculation mud weight for each problematic interval based on fracture gradient, pore pressure and compressive strength of those interval rocks should be performed to prepare suitable mud and overbalanced with minimum 200 psi more than pore pressure.

1 26” Hole Section / 20” Surface Casing

1.1 General

Casing Point 20” Casing @ 150m (20”, 94#, K-55, BTC)Formation AghajariRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems No Significant Hole ProblemDeviation control TotcoLogging ---Drilling FluidBentonite MudMW 65-68 pcfVis. 70-80 secPH ≥ 10.5Drilling ParametersWOB Maximum AvailableGPM 600-1100RPM 90-130

1.2 Formation Sampling

On each 5 meters one sample would be taken. In addition to this general rule, geologist may decide to take more samples.

1.3 Sequence of Operation

 Make up 26” BHA as per BHA design, RIH.

 Tag bottom of cellar, start controlled drilling. Drill the first and second stands at

60-70 RPM with low flow rate (300 GPM) to avoid washing out the conductor

pipe.

 Pump Hi-Vis pill to sweep hole when every stand drilled down. Continue drilling to

±150 m (20”casing point in Aghajari formation). Pay enough attention to drill

straight hole. Use LCM pill if encountered heavy losses. If loss couldn’t be cured by

LCM pill next program will be issue later.

Note:

- TD should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole under casing shoe.

- The exact TD will be determined based on 20”casing tally.

- Do not forget to strap pipe while POOH.

- Run Totco each 50 m. to evaluate the verticality of hole.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 50

- Use following parameters to drill this hole section:

-WOB = Maximum available {Pay enough attention to drill straight hole}

-RPM = 90-130

-GPM = 600-1100

 At TD, Circulate hole clean. POOH to surface. Check bit if balling. Perform

condition trip. Spot Hi-Vis pills after circulate hole clean. Drop Totco, POOH and

retrieve survey tool.

 Hold pre-job safety meeting with involved personnel for running 20″ casing.

 R/U 20” casing running gear &amp;amp; function test 20″ casing running equipment. Perform

Safety meeting.

 Match the stab-in stinger with float shoe on surface.

 P/U and M/U shoe joint.

 Thread-lock shoe connection. Function test float shoe, Fill up casing with mud every

joint to keep weight balance between inside casing and annular. Monitor WOH

during running casing to detect excessive drag.

 Run ±150m (Approx. 15 Jts) 20", 94 ppf, K-55, BTC casing with stab-in type float

shoe to bottom as per casing program (install centralizers as per casing tally). Casing

shoe should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough stick

up (1.5 m) on rig floor. Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud joint by joint.

 Rig up C-plate. Run stab-in stinger (with new O-ring seal), 5″ pup joint (with 5″ x

20″ bow spring centralizer) and 5″ HWDP as stinger string in 20″ casing

 Slowly lower stinger string and make sure stab into float shoe ok.

 Make up circulating head. Fill up annulus (5″ x 20″) with mud and check for sealing.

 Secure the inner string to the rig substructure with slings.

 Break circulation slowly. And circulate 200% of annular volume with mud at 800-

1000 GPM.

Note:

- Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or ledges are encountered.

 Hook up cement line and pressure test same (Test Pressure: 3000 psi).

 Hold pre job safety meeting with related people.

 Cement 20″ casing as per cement program.

 P/U stinger, circulate to flush string inside. POOH.

 WOC.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 51

Note:

- Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to perform top-up jobs.

 If there is no cement return to surface, a top-up job should be performed to fill the

annulus, stage by stage until cement reaches the surface. Use the same weight as the

primary cement slurry. Ensure adequate X-Over for connecting line pipe to cement

line is available. Wait for the top job cement to get hard.

 Correctly determine elevation from top flange of CHH to RKB and then Rough cut

20” landing joint and L/D same. L/D and remove 30” conductor pipe.

 Dress casing neck suitable to install and to weld casing head spool. (If C.H.H is slip

type at bottom, install it as per Wellhead Engineer Installation).

 Install and weld casing head housing 20 3/4” – 3000 psi on casing stub and test the

sealing of welded casing (limit pressure to a maximum of 80% of the collapse

strength of the casing or maximum 400 psi). Outlets to align with cellar stairs.

 N/U spacer, kill/choke manifold and 20-3/4″ 3K Hydrill.

 P/T wellhead &amp;amp; BOP system as per NIOC Policy.

 Install wear bushing.

Note:

- Do not forget to check the wear bushing with CHH and tie down flange before N/U BOP’s.

 Fill up the following forms and send to drilling office:

20” Casing Tally

20” Casing Report

20” Cement Report

26” Hole Section Summary

Survey Report

WHD Test report &amp;amp; it’s diagram including dimensions and serial numbers.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 52

2 17-1/2” Hole Section / 13-3/8” Intermediate Casing

2.1 General

Casing Point 13-3/8” Casing @ 1449m (68#, N-80, BTC)Formation Aghajari, Gachsaran, 5 m above top of AsmariRisks &amp;amp; Hole problems Loss, Salt water influx, Calcium contamination &amp;amp; tight holeLogging GR/CALI/Deviation Tool (Single shot every 100m and multi shot every 300m)Drilling Fluid(1)Bentonite Mud (F/150 TO 300 m.)MW 65-68 pcfVis. 70-80 [[Sec.PH](http://Sec.PH)]([http://Sec.PH](http://Sec.PH)) ≥ 10.5Drilling Fluid(2)KCL/Polymer Mud (F/300 TO 1449m.)MW 72-84 pcfVis. 40- 50 [[Sec.PH](http://Sec.PH)]([http://Sec.PH](http://Sec.PH)) 9.5 - 10Drilling ParametersWOB 10 - 50 klbRPM 90-150GPM 900-1100

2.2 Formation Sampling

From 150m to section TD: On each 2-5 meters one sample would be taken.

Note: As per well site geologist request, the sample program may be changed.

2.3 Sequence of Operations

 M/U and RIH with 17-1/2″ drilling BHA.

 Tag TOC. Circulate. Drill out cement and shoe track with reduced parameters.

 Drill with reduced parameters till stabilizer has passed through casing shoe.

 Continue drill 3-5m new formation.

 Circulate hole clean It. POOH to casing shoe.

 Perform FIT as per procedure (EMW= Max hole MW+2 PCF).

 Continue drilling to 300 m, Displace new mud into hole.

 Drill ahead with optimized parameters.

 Short trip every 300m or depending on hole condition.

 Drill hole to section TD, perform wiper trip, circulate, spot Hi-Vis pill if necessary,

take survey and POOH.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 53

 Use following parameters to drill this hole section:

-WOB = 10-50 klb

-RPM = 90-150

-GPM= 1100-900

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 Conduct Wire-line logging as program.

 If the hole dictates, Install wear bushing and perform condition trip for running

casing.

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 R/U casing running tools and equipment.

 P/U and M/U float shoe and float collar, Thread-lock all shoe track connections.

 Function test floating equipment.

 Run 13-3/8″ casing (68#, N-80, BTC) and install centralizers as per tally.

 Fill up casing every 5-10 joints. Monitor hook loads during running of the casing to

detect excessive drag.

 Break circulation when run to mid depth of borehole.

 Tag bottom to confirm casing setting depth.

 Rig up cementing head, connect cement line and pressure test same (Test Pressure:

3000 psi).

 Circulate at least one bottom up or till clean the hole.

 Hold safety meeting with related persons while circulating.

 Cement 13-3/8″ casing as cement program.

 If observe bumping, increase pressure to 1500 psi and hold it for 15 min. &amp;amp;

report as casing integrity test.

 If observed no slurry return to surface, top-fill job is required.

 Set 13-3/8″ casing hanger in CHH with required tension.

Note:

13-3/8” casing slips should be tested to 800-1000 psi

 L/D cementing head &amp;amp; landing joint.

 N/D 20-3/4″,3K Hydrill and Spacer Spool

 N/U 20-3/4″ 3k x 13-5/8″ 5K C.H.S. and P/T p-seals &amp;amp; Cavity test (80% of casing

collapse pressure rate).

 N/U 13-5/8”- 10K BOP stack.

 P/T BOP system and C.H.S.

 Retrieve test plug and install wear bushing.

Note:

-Perform BOP's test on each 14 days as per NIOC Policy.

-Adjust mud weight with respect to the hole condition.

- Casing shoe should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

-POOH. If observed any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream to make sure that the hole condition is good.

3 12-1/4” Hole Section / 9-5/8” Production Casing

3.1 General

Casing Point 9-5/8” Casing@ 3933m MD (47#, 110S, TPCQ)Formation Asmari, Pabdeh, Gurpi, Ilam, Laffan, Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Darian, Gadvan.Risks &amp;amp; Hole problemsTight Hole, Hole stability problem, Salt water influx, Pipe sticking, High pressure salt water contamination, Calcium contamination, H2S Risk, Gas Flow, Complete loss.LoggingGR/DT/Deviation Tool (From Asmari to top of Ilam), Full Suite/DSI (From Ilam to top of Fahliyan), Deviation Tool (Single shot every 100m and multi shot every 300m), CBL/VDL/CCL/GRDrilling FluidKcl-pol-Glyc MudMW 76 -86 PCFVis. 40-50 Sec/[[Q.PH](http://Q.PH)]([http://Q.PH](http://Q.PH)) 9.5-10Drilling ParametersWOB 10- 40 KlbRPM 90-150GPM 600-800PDC bit drilling parameters will be issued in due time.

3.2 Formation Sampling

From 1449 m to section TD: on each 2-5 meters one sample would be taken.

Note: As per well site Geologist request, the sample program may be changed.

3.3 Sequence of Operations

 M/U &amp;amp; RIH 12-1/4″ BHA to tag TOC.

 Circulate. Drill plugs, float collar and cement to 7m above float shoe with reduced

parameters.

 Displace new mud and circulate to condition same.

 If observed NO bumping while 13 3/8” cement job, Circulate and uniform mud then

performed P/T 13 3/8” casing integrity test to 1500 PSI.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 56

 Drill out cement shoe track, shoe and cement of rat hole; be careful for any

indication of abnormal torque, since there might be a possibility of shoe being

backed off. In case, report immediately.

 Continue drill 3-5m new formation.

 Circulate hole clean It. POOH to casing shoe.

 Perform FIT as per procedure (EMW= Max hole MW+2 PCF).

 Drill ahead with optimized drilling parameter.

 Perform short trip every 300m or depending on hole condition.

 Continue Drill to section TD, perform wiper trip, circulate, spot Hi-Vis pill if

necessary.

Use following parameters to drill this hole:

-WOB = 10 - 40 Klbs

-RPM = 90-150 RPM

-GPM= 800-600

Note: PDC bit drilling parameters will be issued in due time.

 POOH.

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 Perform wire line logging according to logging program.

 If the hole dictates, Install wear bushing and perform condition trip for running

casing (adjust mud weight if needed to run 9-5/8” casing).

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 Change rams to 9 5/8” and perform function &amp;amp; body test.

 R/U casing running tools include jam system, PJSM for running casing.

 P/U and M/U float shoe and float collar, Thread lock all shoe track joints.

 Function test floating equipment.

 Run 9-5/8″ casing (47#, 110S, TPCQ) and install centralizers as per casing tally.

 Fill up casing every 10 joints.

 Break circulation at previous casing shoe.

 Tag bottom and P/U to confirm the casing setting depth.

 P/U string to have 1m rate hole.

 Rig up cementing head, connect cementing line and pressure test same (Testing

pressure: 5000 psi).

 Slowly break circulation and circulate hole clean with optimal flow rate.

 Cement 9-5/8″ casing as per cementing program.

 If observe bumping, increase pressure to 3000 psi and hold it for 15 min. &amp;amp;

report as casing integrity test.

 If no slurry returns to surface, top-fill job is assumed.

 Set 9-5/8″ casing hanger in 20-3/4″ 3k x 13-5/8″ 5k CHS with required tension as

per Wellhead engineer instruction.

Note:

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 57

9-5/8” casing slips should be tested to 2000 psi.

 R/D cementing head &amp;amp; landing joint.

 N/U 13-5/8″ x 11″ 10K T.H.S and P/T port seals &amp;amp; Cavity test (80% of casing

collapse pressure rate).

 N/U 13-5/8”- 10K BOP stack.

 M/U test plug and set in THS. Ensure outlet valve of casing head spool below test

plug is open.

 P/T all ram preventers, choke and kill manifolds, annular preventer, all surface BOP

equipment including IBOP and safety valves as NIOC Policy.

 Retrieve test plug and install wear bushing.

Note:

- Perform BOP's test on each 7 days as per NIOC policy.

- Casing shoe should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

- Adjust mud weight with respect to the hole condition.

- Section TD may be changed based on formation tops detected by well site geologist.

- Do not forget to strap pipe while POOH.

4 8 1/2” Hole Section / 7” Production Liner

4.1 General

Liner point 7” Liner @ 4125m MDLiner specification: 7”, 32#, 110S, TPCQ.Formation Fahliyan.Risks &amp;amp; Hole problems Loss of circulation, Tight hole, Pipe sticking, H2S and poisonous gas. Pipe twisting off.Logging GR/Deviation Tool (Single shot every 100m and multi shot every 300m), Full Suite/DSI &amp;amp; CBL/VDL/CCL/GR.Drilling FluidGlycol/KCL/Polymer MUDMW 100-106 pcfVis. 45-55 Sec/QPV 28-38cpYP 15-22 Lb/100ft²Drilling ParametersWOB 10-30 KlbRPM 90-150GPM 400-600PDC bit drilling parameters will be issued in due time.

4.2 Formation Sampling

On each 2-5 meters one sample would be taken. In addition to this general rule, geologist may decide to take more samples.

4.3 Sequence of Operations

 M/U 8-1/2″ drilling BHA, RIH to tag TOC.

 Drill out cement to 7m above float shoe with reduced parameters.

 Displace new mud and circulate to condition same.

 If observed NO bumping while 9 5/8” cement job, Circulate and uniform mud then

performed P/T 9 5/8” casing integrity test to 3000 PSI.

 Continue drill out cement and shoe.

 Commence drilling formation with reduced parameters.

 Continue drill 3-5 m of formation.

 Circulate clean the hole. POOH to casing shoe.

 Perform FIT as per procedure, (EMW= Max hole MW+2 PCF).

 Continue drill about 10m new formation. Circulate hole clean, POOH.

 Perform CBL/VDL/CCL/GR logging.

 M/U 8-1/2″drilling BHA, RIH to BTM.

 Drill 8-1/2″ hole with optimized parameters to TD.

 Wiper trip will be performed once every 300m footages drilled or depending on hole

condition.

 Continue Drill to TD.

 Perform Condition Trip prior to Logging.

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 Perform wire line logging.

 Install wear bushing.

Note:

- Short trip shall be performed once every 300m footages drilled or depending on hole condition.

- TD shall be confirmed by the well site Geologist.

- Circulate hole clean with maximum flow rate as possible at TD.

 Wiper trip to 9-5/8″ casing shoe. RIH to bottom, record and wipe out any tight spots.

 Circulate hole clean with maximum flow rate as possible, spot Hi-Vis if necessary.

 POOH. Perform flow check before pull the bit above the previous shoe.

 Condition trip for running 7” liner (adjust mud weight if needed to run 7” liner).

 Retrieve wear bushing.

 R/U 7” casing running tools include jam system, PJSM for running liner.

 P/U and M/U 7” float shoe, 7” float collar and 7” LDC, Thread lock connections of

all shoe track joints.

 Function test floating equipment.

 Run 7″ liner and install centralizers as per liner tally.

 Fill up Liner every 10 joints to full.

 M/U liner hanger with top packer. Cont. running 7″ liner on DP.

 Fill up pipe every 5 stands. Break circulation when running to 9-5/8″ casing shoe.

 Tag bottom and P/U to confirm the liner setting depth.

 P/U string to have 1m rate hole.

 Hold PJSM for cementing.

 R/U cementing head and connect cementing line (Testing pressure:5000 psi)

 Slowly break circulation and circulate hole clean with optimal flow rate.

 Set liner hanger and release running tool following the instruction of L.H engineer.

 Cement liner as per program (If decided to suspend the well after running 7” liner,

displace cement slurry by Hi-PH mud PH: 11).

 If observe bumping, increase pressure to 3000 psi and hold it for 15 min. &amp;amp;

report as casing integrity test.

 P/U setting tools, Circulate to observe cement on surface. Continue circulate to clean

the hole.

 Set liner packer.

 POOH. L/D liner setting tools.

 P/U clean out BHA, RIH and clean well to liner lap.

 C.B.U to clean the hole, POOH 30 m above 7” Liner lap, Closed top rams and

perform liner lap pressure test (Step by Step to 2000 psi for 30 minutes).

 Perform dry test liner lap.

 P/U 5 7/8” clean out BHA.

 If observed NO bumping while cementing:

 Before CBL/VDL logging be sure the inside of 7” liner (Liner lap to Float collar) is

clean &amp;amp; the inside of 7” liner is ready for logging.

 Perform wire line logging (CBL/VDL).

 Perform Scraper Trip (Scraping PKR depth perforation &amp;amp; PKR depth completion).

 POOH &amp;amp; Perform Perforation (the program will represented in due time).

 Run Completion String (the program will represented in due time).

2 26" Hole Section

2.1 Section Discussion

The 26” hole is planned to drill with Bentonite Hi Vis. Mud to Section TD at 150 m to run 20” surface casing.

After drilling of each stand, 40 bbl. of High viscous pill should be pumped to clean hole or more if the hole condition dictates. Once TD is reached, pump 80 bbl. of high viscous pill, circulate it out and prior to pull out the BHA for running the 20" casing,

General practices to help avoiding hole to pack off during connection:

 Circulate out the whole with 100 bbl. Hi-Vis pill prior to any Totco run in hole

 Prior to POOH for BHA change, spot sufficient amount of Hi-Vis pill at bottom

3. Add 25-35lb/bbl. of bentonite and allow hydrating for as long as possible

4. Raise PH to ±11 with additional of caustic soda (1-2 lb. /bbl.)

3 17 1/2" Hole Section

3.1 Section Discussion

The 17 1/2” hole section will be drilled from 20" casing shoe at 150 ± m to an estimated depth of 1449± m. The interval consists of two formations, Aghajari, Gachsaran formations. The formations are predominately made up of Sandstone, Clay stone, Marl, and anhydrate.

This section will be drilled down using bentonite mud to 300 m from previous section. After drilling 300 m interval, mud should be dumped and the hole will be displaced with KCl / Polymer system and drilling continues to the depth of 1449m (MD).

Before entering Gachsaran formation density should reach to 75 PCF and fluid loss control is the critical issue while drilling this section and should be maintained as program.

3.2 Drilling Hazards

Wellbore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

* Swelling clays

*Partial losses

3.3 Hole instability

In addition to KCl mud and PHPA can be added to provide extra inhibition in clay section. PHPA acts as encapsulating polymer, minimizing the hydration dispersion of cutting. Pump low Vis and hi-vis. pill periodic

3.4 Lost circulation

The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. In the event of seepage losses, treat system with a fine to medium grade of LCM at 10 lb. /bbl. to 20 lb. /bbl. In the event of moderate to high rate of wellbore loss, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot over thief zone. Concentration of LCM may need to recalculate.

3.6 Mixing procedures

3.6.1 Interval of 150-300 m

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water

2. Treat out any hardness greater than 600 ppm with soda ash (1-3b/bbl).

3. Add 25-30lb/bbl. of bentonite and allow hydrating for as long as possible

4. Raise PH to 10.5- 11.5 with additional of caustic soda (0.5-2 lb. /bbl.)

3.6.2 Interval of 300-1449 m

After drilling up to 300 m, displace the previous mud with the new mud:

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water

2. Add 0.3%-0.5% anti-bacterial

3. Treat out any hardness greater than 800 ppm with soda ash 1-3 ppb

4. Add KCl for shale inhibitor to reach %4-%6 w/w about 14.4-21.6 ppb.

5. Add NaCl to M.W 74 PCF.

6. Add Green starch 9 -11 ppb then wait 10 to 15 minute on mixing

7. Use 1-2 ppb PAC-L and wait 5 minute

8. Use 0.5-1 ppb PAC-R and wait 5 minute

9. Use Limestone to weight up above 74 PCF as needed

10. Add about 0.5-2 ppb caustic soda to control pH and reduce hardness, PH value should not

exceed more than 10.

Notices

1. Make PHPA pill in reserve tank w/0.5 - 1 ppb solution and add to system while drilling

2. If use of potato starch cannot control F.L replace it with Modified or HT starch.

3. At 900 MT F.L should be reduce to 10 cc/30 min.

4. Use solid control equipment while drilling (Especially the centrifuges).

5. Add materials one by one as shown same as above.

6. Pump Low Vis and Hi vis. pill as need.

4 12 1/4" Hole Section

4.1 Section Discussion

The 12 1/4" hole section will be drilled from 13 3/8" casing shoe at 1449± m to an estimated section TD of 3933± m (MD).

The 9 5/8" casing shall cover Asmari, Pabdeh, Gurpi, Ilam, Laffan, Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Darian, and Gadvan formations. The fluid from previous section will be carried and utilization of a Glycol/ KCl / Polymer mud system (add glycol to active previous mud system) and should firmly inhibit shale and provides excellent properties drilling and inhibition properties.

4.2 Drilling Hazards

Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

* Hole instability

* Salt water influx

4.2.1 Hole Instability

It is important to maintain mud weight as per pore pressure curve dictates in the drilling program. Ensure the concentrations are as per program and that these are determined and reported every tour. This should provide optimum hole instability and less hole problem.

4.2.2 High formation loss

If observe high amount of loss in formation, drilling should be performed in By-Pass mode. Additionally, LCMs are also added to the mud system. After finishing drilling of hi loss formation zone maybe pumping sodium silicate and cement slurry should be performed as per instruction.

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water

2. Treat out any hardness greater than 700 ppm with soda ash

3. Add KCl for shale inhibitor to reach %7 - 8% w/w (about 25.6 – 29 ppb)

4. Add NaCl to weight up fluid to 74PCF

5. Add 10 - 12 ppb starch HT and let to be mixed for 10 to 15 minute

6. use 1 - 2 ppb PAC-L and mix it for at least 5 minute

7. use Limestone to weight up above 74 PCF as needed

8. add 0.5 - 1 ppb Xc-polymer as needed

9. Shall use %3 - %5 v/v Glycol to active system. (Before using coordinate with office)

10. Add about 0.3 - 1 ppb caustic soda to control pH and reduce hardness, PH value should not

exceed more than 10.

Notice:

1. Use solid control equipment while drilling (Especially the centrifuges).

2. If need Add Bit Lubricant solution (%2 - %3 v/v), (Before use coordinate with office).

5 8 1/2" Hole Section

5.1 Section Discussion

The 8 1/2" hole section will be drilled from 9 5/8" casing shoe at 3933 ± m to an estimated section TD of 4125 ± m (MD). The 7" liner shall cover Fahliyan formation. The fluid from previous section will be carried and utilization of a KCl/Glycol/Polymer mud system and increase mud weight for 8 ½” hole and should firmly inhibit and provides excellent properties drilling and inhibition properties.

5.2 Drilling Hazards

Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

* Possible losses

* Present of H2S

5.2.1 Lost circulation

The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Partial to severe losses are expected in Sarvak formation and therefore sufficient stocks of acid soluble graded LCM, such as fine CaCO3 and oyster shell should be kept on board. In case of seepage losses, the system should be treated with medium grade of LCM. For moderate to severe losses, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot across the thief zone.

5.2.2 Presence of H2S

Treat system with H2S scavenger, keep high alkalinity and use Garret gas train to determine concentration.

6 Solid control

1) Each kind of solid control equipment should be designed to process 100% to 150% of the

maximum anticipated circulating volume of mud. Shale shaker, de-sander, de-silter and cleaner

are required to run all the time during drilling and centrifuge is recommended to run at least 80%

of drilling time.

2) The sand content and MBT should be kept as low as possible by solid removal and dilution. Batch

wise dilution (replacement of 20-30% of the total drilling fluid volume in one circulation) is more

effective than continuous dilution.

3) The detailed requirement of solid control equipment are as follows:

7 Guidelines for Kill mud preparation

1) The kill fluid should be prepared prior to commence of each hole section, If well flow observed. The density of kill fluid is determined usually heavier than the formation coefficient should be recalculated this coefficient by new situation. Volume of kill fluid is following the regulation of South Azadegan Oil Field:

For pore pressure coefficient of formation increase and pay zone without H2S, the volume of kill mud will changed and need to recalculate.

If in case pore pressure coefficient of formation increase and pay zone has H2S, the volume of kill mud will changed and need to recalculate.

2) The following emergency or contingency materials should be kept on the rig site at all times to supplement the proposed mud system. Kick, lost circulation, stuck pipe scenarios, lubrication and general purpose operations may require the following products to be on storage or standby.

Kill Fluid and Chemical Storage

Section (inch) 26 17½ 12 ¼" 8 ½"Max Pore P. coefficient 1.18 1.26 1.26 1. 6LCM (Tone) - 15 15 10Pipe Lax (DR) - 10 10 10

8 Formation or Pore Pressure and Mud Weight

Primary Well Control relies on the hydrostatic pressure of drilling fluid to balance the formation pore pressure. In order to counteract any minor pressure reductions on bottom due to swabbing effect when tripping, it is customary to maintain a hydrostatic column with a pressure slightly exceeding the pore pressure of the exposed formation. This overbalance, safety margin, or trip margin, Ptm, varies depending on circumstances but it is generally of the order of 200 psi for conventional operations. The required density Pf to control a formation at a depth, D, with a pressure, Po, is given by: Pf = (Po+Ptm), or Df = (Po+Ptm)/D

To gas cut or contaminated mud, Df = Do + (0.07-0.15) sg,

To oil and/or salt water contaminated mud, Df = Do + (0.05-0.10) sg;

4 Casing running procedure

4.1 26” Hole Section / 20” Surface Casing

String Design

 20” Casing Stab-in Float Shoe, 94 ppf, K-55, BTC.

 ±150m (Approx.13 Jts) 20” Casing, 94 ppf, K-55, BTC. (Est. length for each joint of CSG=12m) to

Surface.

 26” x 20” bow Centralisers on each 2 Joints.

Preparation

 Visually and mechanically check the Stab-in Float Shoe for proper operation.

 Ensure all connections are thoroughly cleaned using steam or a grease solvent.

 Ensure that all casing running equipment are available and in good operation condition.

 Ensure the correct casing drifts are supplied to the rig.

 Ensure that all casing joints have been drifted.

 Ensure all cement bulk lines have been checked for blockage with bulk tanks fluffed and pressurized to the

required transfer pressure.

 Inspect and pressure test cement manifold.

Sequence of Operations

 Conduct safety meeting with all crews involved.

 Rig up casing handling and running equipment.

 P/U 20”, 94 ppf, K-55, BTC, float shoe, soft make up on first joint, P/U and make up to the optimum

torque. Do not engage the tong close to bottom of shoe. Use thread lock compound for shoe.

 Run 20” casing &amp;amp; centralisers according to tally.

 Run casing at speed calculated by WSS. A rate of 5 Joints/hr. could be used as a guide.

 Work slightly through any tight spots if necessary. Circulate if deemed necessary.

 Fill casing while running in each joint.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 104

4.2 17-1/2" Hole Section / 13 3/8” Intermediate Casing

String Design

 13-3/8” Casing float Shoe, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC.

 Two Joints (Approx. ±24m) of 13-3/8” Casing, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC.

 13-3/8” Casing float Collar, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC.

 ±1425m (Approx. ±119Jts) of 13-3/8” Casing, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC. (Est. length for each joint of CSG=12m).

 Bow Centralizer on the first 4 joints used every joint.

 Bow Centralizers 1 ea. every four joints.

 Rigid centralizer on third and fourth joints from top.

Preparation

 Visually and mechanically check the float shoe and collar for proper operation.

 Ensure all connections are thoroughly cleaned using steam or a grease solvent.

 Ensure that all casing running equipment are available and in good condition.

 Ensure the correct casing drifts are supplied to the rig.

 Check the plugs fins not to be squeezed or damaged.

 Ensure that all casing joints have been drifted. Including 13 3/8”swedge (If required to wash casing down).

 Ensure all cement bulk lines have been checked for blockage with bulk tanks fluffed and pressurized to the

required transfer pressure. Ensure that the air supply for cement is functioning properly.

 Inspect and pressure test cement manifold.

Sequence of operations

 Rig up casing handling and running equipment.

 M/U 13 3/8”, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC, float shoe. If it is not already made up on joint, soft make up on first

joint, P/U and make up to the optimum make up torque based on triangle using casing tong. Do not engage

the tong close to bottom of shoe. Repeat the same for collar.

 Use thread locking compound for shoe track plus two joints on top of float collar. Take special care on

thread locking these joints, since the consequence of improper locking would be disastrous.

 Run two joints of 13 3/8”, 68 ppf, N-80, BTC casings.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 105

 Make up 13 3/8”, 68 ppf, BTC, float collar.

 Run Approx. ±1425m (±119 Jts.) of 13 3/8” Casing, 68ppf, N-80, BTC. (Est. length for each joint of

CSG=12m).

Note: Circulate through casing after running of totally 5 Jts. for one Casing capacity.

 Note: Manage to have one meters of rat hole and 1.5 meters of casing stick up on rig floor.

 Pump one and half the casing volume @ 20” shoe and on TD.

 Run casing at speed calculated by Company Man. a rate of 10 Joints/hr. could be used as a guide.

 Using stabbing board &amp;amp; single joint elevator to run casing would be time saving.

 Work slightly through any tight spots. Circulate if deemed necessary.

 Precautionary wash the last joint.

 When landed the casing, M/U cement head. Load the plugs accordingly. Check the flag to operate properly.

4.3 12-1/4" Hole Section / 9 5/8” Production Casing

String Design

 9 5/8” Casing float Shoe, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 Two Joints (Approx.±24m) of 9 5/8” Casing, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 9 5/8” float Collar, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 ±3909m (Approx. ±326 Joints) of 9 5/8” Casing, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ (Est. length for each joint of

CSG=12m).

 Bow Centralizer on the first 4 joints.

 Bow Centralisers 1 ea every four joints.

 Rigid centralisers on third and fourth joints from top.

Preparation

 Visually and mechanically check the shoe &amp;amp; collar for proper operation.

 Ensure all connections are thoroughly cleaned using steam or a grease solvent.

 Ensure that all casing running equipment is available and in good condition.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 106

 Ensure the correct casing drifts are supplied to the rig.

 Check the plugs fins not to be squeezed or damaged.

 Ensure that all casing joints have been drifted, including 9 5/8”swedge (If required to wash casing down).

 Ensure all cement bulk lines have been checked for blockage with bulk tanks fluffed and pressurized to the

required transfer pressure. Ensure that the air supply system for cement is functioning properly.

 Inspect and pressure test cement manifold.

Sequence of Operations

 Rig up casing handling and running equipment, (Including jam system).

 P/U 9 5/8 float Shoe, 47 ppf,110S, TPCQ, and M/U to the optimum make up torque. Do not engage the

tong close to bottom of shoe.

 Use thread locking compound for shoe track plus two joints on top of collar.

 Run two joints of 9 5/8”, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ, casing.

 Make up 9 5/8”, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ, float collar.

 Run Approx. ±3909m (±326 Joints) of 9 5/8”, 47 ppf, 110S, TPCQ casing.

Note: Circulate through casing after running of totally 5 Jts for one Casing capacity.

 Manage to have 1m of rat hole and 1.5 meters of casing stick up on rig floor.

 Pump a complete casing volume at shoe and on the section TD.

 Run casing at speed calculated by Company Man. A rate of 10 Jts/hr. could be used as a guide.

 Work slightly through any tight spots if necessary. Circulate if deemed necessary.

 Precautionary wash the last joint.

 When landed the casing, M/U cement head. Load the plugs accordingly. Check the flag to operate properly.

4.4 8 1/2" Hole Section / 7” Liner

String Design

 7” Float shoe, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 Two Joints (Approx.±24m) of 7” Liner, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 7” Float Collar, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 One Joint (Approx.±12m) of 7” Liner, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 107

 7” LDC, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 ±262m (Approx. ±22 Joints) of 7” Liner, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ. (Est. length for each joint of Liner=12m).

 7” x 9 5/8” Liner Hanger.

 Liner hanger setting tools.

 Approximately 3863m of 5” DP to surface.

 Rotating Centralizer on the first 4 joints used every joint.

 Rotating centralizer every 3 joints.

 7” Rotating centralizer on the third, fourth, fifth and sixth joint from the top.

Preparation

 Visually and mechanically check the shoe &amp;amp; collar for proper operation.

 Check that all consumable liner equipment, running tools, darts and plugs are on location.

 Ensure all connections are thoroughly cleaned using steam or a grease solvent.

 Ensure that all casing running equipment is available and in good condition.

 Ensure the correct casing drifts are supplied to the rig.

 Check the plugs fins not to be squeezed or damaged.

 Ensure all cement bulk lines have been checked for blockage with bulk tanks fluffed and pressurized to the

required transfer pressure. Ensure that the air supply system for cement is functioning properly.

 Inspect and pressure test cement manifold.

Sequence of Operations

1. WSS is to supervise the loading of liner wiper plug in liner running tool, and loading of drill pipe

wiper dart in cementing head.

2. The liner hanger engineer is to be on the rig floor at all times that the liner is being run in open hole.

3. Hold pre-job safety meeting. When running-in, apply thread lock to all connections of the shoe track.

4. Rig up liner running equipment. ,(Including jam system).Run 7” liner string considering the

following arrangements:

 7”, 32ppf, 110S, TPCQ., float shoe.

 Two joints (Approx. 24m) of 7”, 32ppf, 110S, TPCQ..

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 108

 7”, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ., float collar.

 One joints (Approx. 12m) of 7”, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ.

 7”, 32 ppf, 110S, TPCQ. Latch down collar.

 Approx. ±262m (±22 Joints) 7”, 32ppf, 110S, TPCQ Liner.

(Est. length for each joint of liner=12m).

 Hydraulic set liner hanger + liner top packer.

 Liner setting sleeve C/W liner setting tool connected

 5”, 19.5 lb/ft., drill pipe to surface.

Centralizers:

- 1 rotating centralizers for the first 4 joints (1ea per joint).

- 1 rotating centralizer per 3 joint for the rest of the open hole.

- 1 rotating centralizer per 3 joint for the rest liner joints inside 9 5/8” casing.

- Use rotating centralizer on the third and fifth and sixth joint from the top.

Place each centralizer between 2 No. stop collars.

5. After making up the shoe track, lower through rotary table and check that the floats are holding. Fill up

with mud and pick up to check float functionality. Circulate for 10 min.

6. Run liner in at controlled speed to minimize pressure surges (approx.12 joints/hr). Check returns

constantly.

7. Fill up liner completely every 10 joints run.

8. When the required numbers of liner joints have been made up, make up the liner hanger &amp;amp; liner top

packer, running tool as per liner hanger engineer’s instructions.

9. Use Liner setting tool retrievable pack off assembly for positive cement job.

10. Circulate 120% of the liner capacity. (Record Liner weight)

11. Run the liner with 5” drill pipe to the casing shoe. Circulate a minimum of one string capacity before

running into open hole, RIH in the open hole at 4-5 min/stand. Liner hanger engineer should be on rig

floor while running liner into open hole. (Check the loss while RIH if need decrease the speed of the

RIH).SLM &amp;amp; drift the drill pipe while RIH the liner.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 109

Note: before loading the drill pipe wiper plug try to pass it from safety valve.

12. M/U combination plug dropping head to the last joint. Use pup joint, if necessary. Check flag sub for

proper functioning.

13. The hanger will be set 70m above the 9 5/8” casing shoe. Try to avoid setting hanger on casing

coupling. Set the Hanger as per hanger engineer procedures.

14. Ensure that the running tool is released from the liner (Check liner hanger by move string 0.5m up and

down – record string lost weight due to set hanger and note in DDR) before starting to cement.

15. Circulate 120% of the string contents.

16. Cement 7" liner as per cementing program.

17. Displace the cement with the cementing pump to the liner wiper plug. Shear out liner wiper plug

(Record the shearing volume and pressure). Displace combined wiper plugs to latch down collar.

18. Bump the plug to 3000 psi (Record and report final displacement pressure prior to bump the plug). If

there is no bump, do not displace more than 25% of the shoe-track volume. Displace with Cementing

Pump and record cement losses.

19. Hold bumping pressure for 15 minutes and Record it as 7” liner integrity test.

20. Bleed off pressure slowly and check for back-flow.

21. Circulate at least one mud circulation clean the hole. Check the bottom’s up for cement return. In case

of not available packer, check for U tube f/ DPs. N/D cementing head. POOH to 100 m above Est. top

of cement, after flush string &amp;amp; Circulate at least one mud circulation, POOH 4 stands, close the well &amp;amp;

monitor pressure till cement get hard. Open and fill up the well, record mud volume to fill the well.

22. Set packer (If available) as per liner hanger engineer instruction.

23. Clean out well to liner lap with 8-1/2” bit.

24. Circulate and clean hole.

25. Perform positive pressure test liner lap to 2000 psi for 30 min.

26. If observed NO bumping while cementing, D/O 5m of hard cement.

27. Perform Dry test liner lap.

Note:

- Drift Drill pipe while run the liner with suitable Drift.

- Perform injectivity test into 7” liner lap if necessary and inject cement around same.

2-4- Cementing Procedure

1) 1) Clean up tanks and preparing chemicals for mixing fluid.

2) Fill tanks with fresh water for mixing fluids.

3) Before finished running casings with company man approval, start mixing cement additives and

preparing mixing fluids according to below cement additives mixing procedure:

a. Add Antifoam to fresh water and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add CaCl2 and allow mixing for 40 minutes.

c. Add Dispersant and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

4) Before running casing, check the stinger and stab-in shoe &amp;amp; Install stab-in shoe to end of first

casing.

5) After running casing, install stinger to end of first 5" drill pipe.

6) RIH to bottom with 5" drill pipe, before setting the stinger, circulation mud.

7) Set the stinger &amp;amp; circulation mud if observed flowing of mud in casing, try again to setting the

stinger.

8) Hang drill pipe.

9) Pre job safety meeting with involved personnel.

10) Test cement line W/ 3000 psi.

11) Pump spacer.

12) Mix and pump cement slurry.

13) Displace cement with drilling fluid.

14) Sting out and check for float valve working.

15) Circulate to clean out inner string.

16) At the same time the cementer to wash unit and cement line &amp;amp; other.

17) Pull out inner string and lay down stinger.

18) Makes new inventory for cement, additives and equipment.

19) Prepare for next job.

Note:

1. The cementing operations information will be recorded by data acquisition system and

should be submitted.

2. The cement program may change according to the actual condition.

3-4- Cementing Procedure

1) 1) Clean up tanks and preparing chemicals for mixing fluid.

2) Fill tanks with fresh water for mixing fluids (Lead and Tail).

3) Before finished running casings with Company man approval, start mixing cement additives and

preparing mixing fluids according to below Lead cement mixing procedure:

a. Add Antifoam and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add Bentonite and allow mixing for 2 hours.

c. Add Low Temperature Retarder and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

4) Tail cement mixing procedure:

a. Add Antifoam and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add Dispersant and allow mixing for 15 minutes.

c. Add Low temperature retarder and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

5) Before running casing, check cement head specially plunger of released cement plug and put the

top &amp;amp; bottom plug.

6) After running Casing, install circulating swage, start circulation at least one casing cap volume.

7) Pre job safety meeting with involved personnel and arrange with mud engineer to measure drilling

fluid &amp;amp; cement loss/gain during cement job.

8) Stop circulation, connect 13 3/8" cement head and cement line.

9) Test cement line W/ 3000 psi.

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 117

10) Pump spacer then release bottom plug.

11) Mix and pump lead cement slurry.

12) Mix and pump tail cement slurry.

13) Release Top plug while pump 2 bbl fresh water by cement unit.

14) Switch to mud pump and start displacement with mud.

15) Displace cement with drilling fluid, If does not observe Bombing pressure, you will pump

additional displacement liquid (Drilling Fluid) (25% Shoe track volume).

16) Bump the plug, bleed off pressure and check for float shoe. (If back-flow, attempt cure problem

by bleeding off and closing in 3 times. If still not holding, hold surface pressure until back flow

stops.).

17) Makes new inventory for cement, additives and equipment.

18) Prepare for next job.

Note 1: The cement program may change according to the actual condition.

Note 2: If Bumping has observed, Increase pressure &amp;amp; hold 1500 psi pressure in CSG for

15 minute.

Note 3: The cementing operations information will be recorded by data acquisition system and will be submitted.

3-5- Complementary Top Job

If the cement does not reach the surface, a complementary cementing job will be done.

4-4- Single Stage Cementing Procedure

1) Clean up Mud tanks and preparing chemicals for mixing fluid.

2) Fill tanks with water for mixing fluids.

3) Before finished running casings with Co. man approval, start mixing cement additives and

preparing mixing fluids according to below Lead / Tail cement mixing procedure:

Lead Cement mixing procedure:

a. Add Antifoam to fresh water and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add High Temperature Retarder to fresh water and allow mixing for 15 minutes.

c. Add MicroBlock and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

Tail Cement mixing procedure:

a. Add Antifoam to fresh water and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add Fluid Loss Controller and allow mixing for 15 min.

c. Add High Temperature Retarder and allow mixing for 15 min.

d. Add Dispersant and allow mixing for 15 min.

e. Add Anti Gas Migration and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

4) Before running casing, check cement head specially plunger of released cement plug and put

the Top &amp;amp; Bottom plug.

5-4- Liner Cementing Procedure

1- Clean up tanks and preparing chemicals for mixing fluid.

2- Fill tanks with fresh water for mixing fluids (Lead and Tail)

3- Before finished running Liner &amp;amp; 5" Drill Pipe with Co. man approval, start mixing cement

additives and preparing mixing fluids according to below cement mixing procedure

a. Add Antifoam to fresh water and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

b. Add Fluid Loss Controller and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

c. Add Dispersant and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

d. Add High Temperature Retarder and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

e. Add Micro block and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

4- Before running Liner, check Plug Dropping Head assembly Specially Pump down Plug

release Handle.

5- Before Liner shoe reach open hole (@ 9 5/8 shoe), prepare circulation at least one annular

volume.

6- When the Liner shoe reached the bottom ,connected Plug dropping head assembly then start

circulation at least one annular volume.

7- While Last circulation, prepare pre job safety meeting with involved personnel and arrange

with mud engineer to measure drilling fluid &amp;amp; cement loss/gain during cement job.

8- Stop circulation, connect cement line.

9- Tests cement line W/5000 psi.

10- Pump per-flush and spacer.

11- Mix and pump cement slurry &amp;amp; pump it in the well.

12- Release pump down plug.

13- Displace With mud at optimum rate. Slow pump rate 10 Bbl before Pump down Plug reaches

Wiper Plug (Drill Pipe Displacement is ……. Bbl) at +/- …….Psi.

14- Watch for indication of drill pipe plug latching into liner, Wiper Plug on pressure gauge. If

this pressure indication is seen, recalculate total displacement volume. The new displacement

volume will be the actual displacement volume for drill pipe plus the calculated liner volume.

15- Slow pump rate down to 3 bbl/ minute, last 10 bbl of displacement volume.

16- Land Plug with 1000 psi over final displacement pressure.

Note: If plug does not bump, do not displace more than %25 of the calculated shoe track. (Optional)

17- Bleed off pressure and monitor U-Tube return.

18- Monitoring return line for checking Float Collar and Float Shoe.

19- Pick up liner hanger running tool and circulate on top of liner hanger, dump excess cement

and mixture until clean.

21- Rig down cement line.

2) Two directions of drilling spool or and X-mas tree can be choosed to nipple up and install:

string run, tested and acidized. The suspension plan is to set deep plug in landing nipple, close

TRSCSSSV, and close SSV and Christmas tree on surface.

4 Kill and Choke Manifold Sketch

5 Pressure Test

5.1 Accumulator, BOP, Wellhead and Choke/Kill Manifold

 The BOP, wellhead, choke manifold, and related equipment are normally to be tested on 14

days basis. This period can be extended depending on the well operations and the

specifications in the Drilling Program and the applicable Regulations.

 The accumulator is to be tested prior to the first test of the BOP, or any time after repairs are

made to the accumulator unit. The accumulator bottles pre-charge pressure shall be checked

before drilling out the casing shoe or every 4 weeks.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 133

 All the pressure tests shall be performed with both low pressure test, usually 300 psi and high

pressure test, usually to the rated value (for annular BOP is 70% of WP) or as per Drilling

Program. All high pressure tests should be maintained for 10-30 minutes, and low pressure

test 5 minutes, unless the Drilling Program dictates otherwise.

5.2 Casing and Liner

In the below table, casing or liner integrity test pressure, surface SIP, is given when test medium is fresh water. As usual, green test casing integrity after bump plug is recommended in the cement procedure. If rigs use the displacement mud (density &amp;gt;1.0sg) to test casing, the pressure applied on surface cement head should be considered the mud hydrostatic pressure at casing collar And the surface SIP is given as the following formula:

Surface shut-in Pressure = 80% Casing Burst – B (psi)

B=Drilling fluid hydrostatic pressure - fresh water hydrostatic pressure

Notes: the assumption in the formula above is the external fluid of the casing is fresh water(density:1.0sg), in order to simply the calculation the test fluid inside casing is also assumed as

5.3 Wellhead

 For port seal: Install a test pump to the port and inject test fluid and test pressure to which is

less than 80% of casing collapse. Hold and monitor the test pressure for 15 minutes. In the

event of a leak, remove the Wellhead and replace the seals as required. Once a satisfactory test

is achieved, carefully bleed off all test pressure, drain the test fluid and reinstall the dust cap.

 For flange seal: Flange seal and body integrity test will be conducted at the same time when

pressure test for ram BOP together

5.4 LOT/FIT

 LOT will be performed in the first well in every area and in all appraisal wells to determine

the equivalent mud weight at which the formation will take fluid. LOT will be conducted after

drill out 13-3/8″, 9-5/8″ and 7″ shoe track to the first weak zone, especially the sand zone, but

no more than 10 m in the new section hole.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 135

 FIT will be performed in the other wells. The test is not designed to breakdown or fracture

formation, just to test the capacity of formation strength. Shut in pressure in wellhead during

FIT should be the value that the hydrostatic pressure at casing shoe equals to (max mud

weight of this hole section+2 PCF)

6 Well Control during Special operation

The well control while Wireline logging and running casing is important and critical, best practices shall be followed, including but not limited to:

1) Change BOP ram to casing size and pressure test same to 1500 psi of surface SIP or the value of

80% casing burst with fresh water.

2) The crossover for casing connection to drill pipe must available on floor. A good practice is to

keep a joint of DP with casing crossover at lower end and full opened safety valve at upper end to

be ready for picking up on V-door.

3) Prior to Wireline logging, install shooting nipple and pressure test same to again WL BOP to

1500 psi.

4) The cable cutter should be available on rig floor. If kick occurs, cut logging cable and to close

blend BOP is optional

5) The contractor shall perform relative pressure test, drills, safety checks etc as per NIOC/NIDC

Policy during the well operations.

MUD LOGGING PROGRAM:

The Mud logging services for all wells should start from the beginning of 26" hole and will continue to the total depth (TD).

A complete mud logging services for all wells must be provided by the contractor with 24 hours (two working shifts of 2 mud loggers + 1 sample catcher + 1 engineer shall be provided by contractor for each 12 hours shift, consisting of four person as day shift and four at night).

LOGS AND REPORTS

Prepared during each shift on a translucent sepia form or paper print (log scale 1:500 meters or according to COMPANY requirements):

Mud Data (including total gas and chromatograph gas, CO2, H2S) Bit Data

Rate of Penetration

Drilling Parameters

Cutting Percentages – Interpreted Lithology

Visual Porosity

Fluorescence – Oil shows

H2S Presence Formation description Core and Test Indications

Report of pertinent information, deviation, casing shoe etc.

Calcimetry

Shale density

Rate of Penetration

Mud Weight

Mud Pit Volume Variations

Lithology

"d" Exponent

Gas component plot

Formation Pressure Gradient

Fracture Gradient

Mud Temperature Gradient

Total Gas

Shale Density

Shale Factors (sonic transit time, Resistivity)

1 26” HOLE SIZE

Description:

The 26” hole is planned to drill with Bentonite Hi Vis. Mud to Section TD at 150 m to run 20” surface casing.

After drilling of each stand, 40 bbl. of High viscous pill should be pumped to clean hole or more if the hole condition dictates. Once TD is reached, pump 80 bbl. of high viscous pill, circulate it out and prior to pull out the BHA for running the 20" casing,

General practices to help avoiding hole to pack off during connection:

 Circulate out the whole with 100 bbl. Hi-Vis pill prior to any Totco run in hole

 Prior to POOH for BHA change, spot sufficient amount of Hi-Vis pill at bottom

Required material as following table:

2 17 ½” HOLE SIZE

Description:

The 17 1/2” hole section will be drilled from 20" casing shoe at 150 ± m to an estimated depth of 1449± m. The interval consists of two formations, Aghajari, and Gachsaran (5m above Asmari formation). The formations are predominately made up of Sandstone, Clay stone, Marl, and anhydrate.

This section will be drilled down using bentonite mud to 300 m from previous section. After drilling 300 m interval, mud should be dumped and the hole will be displaced with KCl / Polymer system and drilling continues to the depth of 1449m (MD).

7 Notices

1) Ensure the cement quality of corral pit &amp;amp; treatment area and the isolation of waste pit &amp;amp; clear

pit using geo membrane before drilling the well.

2) Ensure the existence and safety of all equipment &amp;amp; machinery for control of waste

management.

3) The cement slurry inside cellar after well cementing should be transferred to the corral pit and

mixing with drilling cutting.

4) The produced waste due to washing the cement track mixer shall be transferred to the corral

pit.

5) Avoid using too much water for washing the mud pumps, tanks and the rig site.

6) Tipper truck should be checked in order to prevent any fluid leakage.

7) Mud and waste management engineers should cooperate in some operations like washing the

mud tanks and using diaphragm pumps.

8) 50% of recycled water should be used for making drilling fluid and the remainder will be used

to wash the equipment and rig site and etc.

9) Mud and waste management engineers should fill in the forms related to equipment according

to fluid properties and work hour of equipment.

10) Record the fluid volume which is made daily.

11) Record the dewatered fluid volume used for daily mud making and daily washing separately.

12) Produced Solids must be fixed using Gypsum (except solids of 26"&amp;amp; first part of 17 1/2"Holes

sections)

13) Carry out quality control tests on fixed cuttings (sheen test &amp;amp; can test)

14) Transfer fixed solids to disposal area at minimum possible time with regard to the prevention

of environmental pollution.

1 Basic HSE Requirements

1. Contractor should establish an effective HSE system and implement it strictly.

2. Contractor must adhere to the standards and regulations of Health, Safe and Environment constituted by

the local government.

3. Contractor must strictly adhere to the procedures / standards and regulations of Health, Safety and

Environment promulgated by NIDC.

4. Contractor should comply with all applicable local government regulations including guidelines and

recommended practices and any other Iranian authorities having jurisdiction.

5. All personnel should respect the local culture, traditions and social values of the indigenous population of

the Iran and South Azadegan region.

6. Contractor shall hire qualified and experienced worker and familiar his personnel with hazards especially

with H2S and Mine and UXO (unexploded ordinance) according to related procedure.

7. Contractor shall conduct proper training about UXO (unexploded ordinance) and mine awareness to all

personnel

2 Well Control

All the drilling operation shall obey the requirement of Well Control Manual of NIDC and contractor’s well control manual; under no circumstances should the deviation from well control policy be permitted without prior approval through NIDC. If the contractor’s well control manual or anywhere has conflict with Well Control Manual of NIDC, take Well Control Manual of NIDC as the standard.

(1) RECORDING REFERENCE

All BOP tests, drills, function tests, any malfunctions, repair or maintenance to the mud system and well control equipment shall be recorded in the IADC daily reports and shall be signed by both the Drilling Contractor's Tool Pusher and Drilling Supervisor on the well site. They shall also be recorded in the Daily Drilling Report.

(2) FLOW CHECK PROCEDURE

To avoid a kick situation, it is recommended that a flow check be conducted at specific times during the drilling operation.

1) While drilling(any warning sign)

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 147

2) Prior to tripping the drill string

3) While tripping(any warning sign)

4) While out of hole(continuously)

5) Trip out:

 After pulling 5% of the drill pipe

 At the midpoint depth of the wellbore

 Prior to pulling the first stand of drill collars

 After all the drill string is out of hole.

6) Trip in:

 Upon reaching surface casing shoe

 At midpoint of the wellbore

(3) PRIMARY CONTROL REFERENCE

It is the responsibility of the Drilling Contractor to keep the hole full at all times by using all the available equipment. The Drilling Supervisor shall monitor that the correct preventive practices are being implemented and/or conducted at all times.

Extreme care shall be taken to monitor mud volume, drilling breaks and gas cut mud.

The MACP (Maximum allowable casing pressure) shall be confirmed by the Drilling Supervisor, either after setting each new casing string or, whenever the density of the drilling mud changes.

The MACP shall be clearly written on a kick control sheet that will be posted near the choke control panel.

The predetermined distance at which the Tool Joint is to be pulled above the rotary should be posted on rig floor near the BOP control panel.

The Driller is responsible for carefully measuring and recording the slow pump pressure. Awareness of these values is an important element in killing operations, in order to avoid formation breakdown.

Take a measure and record the slow pump pressure at three different flow rates with all pumps at the following times:

 Once per tour.

 Whenever changes occur in the mud density/rheology.

 Whenever changes occur in the dimension and characteristics of the string, BHA, jet size, jet plugged

or jet lost, etc.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 148

(4) SECONDARY CONTROL REFERENCE

Any time a drilling break is noticed, drilling shall stop and a static monitoring of the well shall be carried out. Space-out for all Pipe Rams and Annular Preventer must be known to the Driller at all times to keep tool joints clear of annular preventer and rams.

(5) KICK DETECTION PROCEDURE WHILE DRILLING

If there is an indication of a possible kick occurring, the Driller shall follow the kick detection procedures outlined below:

Stop drilling with the pump onPick up top drive to a predetermined space-out positionShut off the pumps and check for well flow by visual examination on the bell nipple or flow line.

1) If the well is not flowing:

 Immediately notify the rig manager, Drilling Supervisor and Tool pusher.

 The Drilling Supervisor will decide whether to circulate bottoms up or resume drilling. The

penetration rate will be limited as long as the cause of the abnormal situation is undetermined.

Always be aware that some hydrocarbons (gas or oil cut mud) may rise to the surface. Ensure that the

proper monitoring and safety systems all in place.

2) If the well is flowing:

 Close the well as per the Shut-in Procedure.

 Immediately notify the Well site Drilling Supervisor, rig manager and Tool pusher.

3 Health

1. This well is committed to develop for oil in a responsible manner that promotes employees'

and local inhabitants' health.

2. A trained Paramedic and proper MEDIVAC equipment and ambulance shall be stationed on

the drilling rig during the drilling campaign.

3. An emergency medical evacuation plan shall be coordinated, and shall keep in touch with

emergency response centres manned by qualified doctor 24-hours per day in Ahwaz city.

4. Medical emergency report and investigation shall be performed as soon as possible in

accordance with Medical Emergency Procedures.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 149

4 Safety

1. Contractor shall make detail operation procedures which have covered all related

activities.

2. Due to high concentration of H2S in Sarvak, Kazhdumi and Gadvan reservoirs, Make

detail H2S operation procedures, in particular, and carry it out strictly.

3. Contractor must provide lifesaving equipment like SCBA, Escape Mask, H2S detectors (Multi

portable and personal detector) and implement the H2S Services system during drilling activity.

4. All lifting equipment and cranes must be certified by approved 3rd party (approved by Institute of

Standards &amp;amp; Industrial Research of Iran)

5. All electrical equipment should be designed for such locations, and listed by nationally recognized

testing laboratory. All wiring components and electrical equipment should be maintained in

accordance with the manufacturers’ recommendation.

6. Contractor shall implement the risk assessment and Job Safety Analysis before starting the drilling

activity.

7. PSSR (Pre-Start up Safety Review) before spud-in and penetrating into oil and gas formations are

mandatory by contractor under the supervision of Company and approved by Company.

8. Contractor shall pay much attention to fire, explosion prevention and well control.

9. Contractor shall make detail Emergency Response Plan and Evacuation Plan.

10. Contractor shall do efforts to keep employees safety as possible.

5 Environment

Requirement on location

1) The access road and well location construction shall be accorded with construction program of

well location. It minimizes any impacts on the local residents, prevent all soil contamination,

prevent all ground water contamination, and prevent all surface water contamination. Cutting and

waste water pit shall be lining and drainage system shall be good and to avoid farm and season

canal contamination.

2) Management regulation for transportation truck, drilling operation machine and tools shall be

carried out strictly to avoid damage farm and trees, etc.

3) Well site shall be flat and clean. All the equipment, materials, drilling tools shall be put in order.

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 150

4) Control the rig noise to decrease the influence to surroundings while drilling at the inhabitation

area.

5) Contractor shall perform on‐site segregation of wastes prior to transfer to the transfer area and/or

delivery to the transportation Contractor in accordance with the following waste hazard

classification.

Non-Hazardous Wastes (Re-usable, Recyclable and Recoverable Wastes)

1) Ferrous and non-ferrous metals, plastics, and wood wastes, PETs and potentially paper and glass

2) Materials shall be separately segregated and provision may be made for size reduction and

containerization equipment.

3) The Contractor/Generators are encouraged to seek for recycle/re-use alternatives based on the

Local available facilities, for final disposal of these materials.

4) It should be noted that landfill disposal of metal waste materials and wood wastes shall be

prohibited. For wood wastes re-use of and/or salvage of commercial timber, or deliver/release to

5) Local community are recommended.

Hazardous Wastes

Hazardous Waste, such as clinic waste, oil waste, waste produced by painting, insulation works shall be duly segregated.

Facilities of environment protection supplied to well site

1) Mud purifying circulation system

2) Cooling water spraying and circulating system for drilling pump

3) Special recovery tank for deposed oil

4) Reserve house for valuable mud chemicals

5) Automatic mud weighting supply system

6) BOP stack is equipped according to drilling design

7) Define a separation area for gathering all waste according to waste management procedure

NIDC

NIDC Drilling Projects Management

Drilling Engineering &amp;amp; Programming Division

South Azadegan Oil field

AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3 PEDEC

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03) Page 151

Requirement of environment protection on location

1) Sewage pit Area of the sewage pit: 1500 m2

2) Prior to spud, mud pit and sewage pit must comply with the standard. Install cooling water

spraying and circulating system for drilling pump and sewage circulating system for cleaning rig

floor and equipment.

3) Install BOP stack and pressure test shall be operated according to the drilling design to avoid

blowout.

4) Install oil supply lines and check whether there is any leakage.

Management of environment protection on location

1) In drilling operation， keep mud and sewage from overflowing. If it happens, clean up timely.

2) Mud chemicals shall be stored according to the standard and shall not be scattered around well

site. If mud chemicals scatter while loading or unloading, clean it up at once. The sack of mud

chemicals shall be recovered. Do not dispose it on site.

3) Enhance water management at well location and do not wash equipment with fresh water.

Stop water flowing for long time. Overhead tanks shall be utilized.

4) Oil management shall be carefully at the well site. No oil dirt on site, no dripping and leaking

at oil tank and manifold system area.

5) After the well is completed, reinforce the sewage pit， solidify the disposed mud and have

environment procedure fulfilled.

6) Install fuel supply lines and keep them in good condition.

7) All the drilling fluid materials shall be recovered and shall not be left at the well site after

completion.

8) The environmental protection personnel from contractor and operator side shall check and

accept the well site environment according to the regulation after completion. Only well site

environment is qualified, the well can be delivered.

9) Transition Area for Hazardous Waste shall also be provided by each Contractor in his

allocated area,

Company shall approve each area before use as well. The transition area of hazardous waste shall

be:

South Azadegan Oil field
